" Test suite categories
" - ltc_utility_unit: strings, dates, formatting and validation (SHORT)
" - ltc_parity_helpers: in-memory JSON/serialization contracts (SHORT)
" - ltcl_invoice_null_propagation: preserved arithmetic regressions (SHORT)
" - ltcl_review_grace_test: review dates and year rollover (SHORT)
" - ltc_amdp_filters: synthetic filter integration on HANA (MEDIUM)
" - ltc_request_processing: filters, sorting/paging, request/package flow
" - ltc_fi_das_gateway_parity: live integration/parity (LONG/HARMLESS)
" - ltc_scope_*: 5 scenarios x 62 scopes, live parity (LONG/HARMLESS)
" - ltc_dash_pure_branches: currency, mapping, context, validation/status
" SHORT tests use in-memory values; MEDIUM/LONG tests call runtime services.

CLASS ltc_fi_das_gateway_parity DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS lcl_scope_parity DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS ltc_parity_helpers DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS ltcl_invoice_null_propagation DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS ltc_dash_pure_branches DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS ltcl_review_grace_test DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS ltc_request_processing DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS ltc_amdp_filters DEFINITION DEFERRED.

CLASS zcl_fi_das_dashboard DEFINITION
  LOCAL FRIENDS ltcl_invoice_null_propagation
                ltc_dash_pure_branches
                ltcl_review_grace_test
                ltc_request_processing
                ltc_amdp_filters.

" XSA vs SEGW functional JSON parity test - paged low-memory implementation
"
" Memory strategy:
" - XSA fixture is indexed once with PO/item and object character offsets only.
" - Raw SEGW JSON is fetched one SKIP/TOP page at a time.
" - JSON properties are read directly from both raw payloads for each matched row.
" - Comparison is strict except for documented, field-specific SEGW mappings of
"   XSA SQL NULL values and a temporary unordered-aggregate compatibility rule.
" - No complete XSA row table, no complete property tree, and no copied JSON
"   objects are held in memory.
"
" Paging relies on the stable ORDER BY implemented by all dashboard flows.

" Integration/parity: requires live Gateway, database and MIME fixtures.
CLASS ltc_fi_das_gateway_parity DEFINITION FINAL FOR TESTING
  DURATION LONG
  RISK LEVEL HARMLESS
  FRIENDS ltc_parity_helpers lcl_scope_parity.

  PRIVATE SECTION.
    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ts_case,
        fixture               TYPE string,
        email                 TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_email,
        poowner               TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_poowner,
        flow                  TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_mode,
        pstype                TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_pstype,
        filter_select_options TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_filter_select_options,
        filter_raw            TYPE string,
        orderby               TYPE string,
        orderby_desc          TYPE abap_bool,
        select_list           TYPE string,
        top                   TYPE i,
        top_specified         TYPE abap_bool,
        inlinecount           TYPE abap_bool,
      END OF ts_case,

      BEGIN OF ts_expected_index,
        ponumber    TYPE string,
        itemno      TYPE string,
        object_from TYPE i,
        object_to   TYPE i,
        matched     TYPE abap_bool,
      END OF ts_expected_index,

      tt_expected_index TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ts_expected_index
        WITH UNIQUE KEY ponumber itemno,

      tt_string TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF string
        WITH EMPTY KEY,

      BEGIN OF ts_json_property,
        name  TYPE string,
        token TYPE string,
      END OF ts_json_property,

      tt_json_property TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ts_json_property
        WITH UNIQUE KEY name.

    CONSTANTS:
      mc_mime_root TYPE string VALUE '/SAP/PUBLIC/DAS/',
      mc_max_log   TYPE i VALUE 5000,

*     Single paging limit for Over, Under and Exclude.
*     Reduce this value when the productive system has a smaller heap.
      mc_page_size TYPE i VALUE 40000.

    DATA:
      mt_differences      TYPE stringtab,
      mv_difference_count TYPE i.

    METHODS setup.

    " Integration: primary flows
    METHODS test_over_material FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_over_service FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_under_material FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_under_service FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_exclude FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.

    " Integration: owner scope, filters, selection and paging

    METHODS test_filter FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_selection FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_y_format FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_x_format FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_empty_format FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_inlinecount_with_top_one FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_no_inlinecount FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_skip_beyond_result_set FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_top_zero FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_po_value_help FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_item_value_help FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_supplier_value_help FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_filter_sort_and_paging FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_under_paging FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_excl_paging FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_exclusion_pstype_desc FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_exclusion_pstype_asc FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_excl_pstype_pages FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_orderby_invalid FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_nonexistent_supplier FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_filter_companycode FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_filter_polinedesc FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.

" Dashboard production branches: in-memory fixtures only, no database access.
" Rates are injected into the cache; dates/timestamps avoid clock and CET paths.
CLASS ltc_dash_pure_branches DEFINITION FINAL FOR TESTING
  DURATION SHORT
  RISK LEVEL HARMLESS.

  PRIVATE SECTION.
    DATA mo_cut TYPE REF TO zcl_fi_das_dashboard.
    DATA ms_request TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request.
    DATA ms_context TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_context.
    DATA ms_detail TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_detail.
    DATA ms_key TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_key.
    DATA ms_row TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_odata_result.

    METHODS setup.
    METHODS read_row RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS assert_rejected
      IMPORTING
        i_msgno TYPE symsgno.

    " Arithmetic and cached currency conversion
    METHODS currency_factors FOR TESTING.
    METHODS usd_truncates_source FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS usd_keeps_precision FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS zero_needs_no_rate FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS missing_rate_rejected FOR TESTING.
    METHODS rate_target_key FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    " Context component mapping
    METHODS detail_mapping FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS account_mapping FOR TESTING.
    METHODS history_mapping FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS user_mapping_guard FOR TESTING.
    METHODS exclusion_mapping FOR TESTING.

    " Row assembly from an injected context
    METHODS row_missing_detail FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS row_material_and_audit FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS row_service_wtd FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS row_optional_context FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS row_exclusion FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS row_uses_complete_key FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS row_closed_status FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS missing_notification_clears FOR TESTING RAISING cx_sy_conversion_error.

    " Scope parsing: singleton wildcard, NULL and normalization
    METHODS company_wildcard FOR TESTING.
    METHODS company_mixed_values FOR TESTING.
    METHODS cost_null_sentinel FOR TESTING.
    METHODS cost_null_and_alpha FOR TESTING.
    METHODS cost_mixed_wildcard FOR TESTING.
    METHODS management_scope FOR TESTING.
    METHODS amount_y_character_path FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    " Validation and request adjustment without filter SQL generation
    METHODS valid_request_modes FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS invalid_mode FOR TESTING.
    METHODS missing_email FOR TESTING.
    METHODS invalid_owner FOR TESTING.
    METHODS invalid_pstype FOR TESTING.
    METHODS negative_paging FOR TESTING.
    METHODS missing_filter_text FOR TESTING.
    METHODS adjust_default_request FOR TESTING.
    METHODS adjust_generic_request FOR TESTING.

    " Status and calculation early-return/boundary branches
    METHODS closed_overrides_status FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS unknown_status_cleared FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS reviewed_without_date FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS material_resets_work FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS unsupported_method FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS straight_line_missing_dates FOR TESTING RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
    METHODS accrued_exact_boundary FOR TESTING.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltc_utility_unit IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD email_normalized.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>normalize_email( '  Mixed.Case@EXAMPLE.COM  ' )
      exp = CONV zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_email( 'mixed.case@example.com' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD email_blank.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>normalize_email( '   ' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD split_default.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>split_list( ', alpha  beta, ,gamma,alpha  beta,' )
      exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_utility=>tt_string(
        ( `alpha beta` ) ( `gamma` ) ( `alpha beta` ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>split_list( '' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD split_custom.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>split_list(
        i_value = ' a,b ; ; c ;' i_delimiter = ';' )
      exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_utility=>tt_string( ( `a,b` ) ( `c` ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>split_list( ', ,,' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD append_default.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = 'a' i_value = `  b  ` )
      exp = `a;b  ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = '' i_value = '' )
      exp = ';' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD append_flags.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = 'old' i_value = ` new ` i_first = abap_true )
      exp = `new ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = 'old' i_value = `  ` i_skip_initial = abap_true )
      exp = 'old' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = '' i_value = 'new' i_skip_initial = abap_true )
      exp = 'new' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = 'a' i_value = 'b' i_delimiter = ','
        i_skip_initial = abap_true )
      exp = 'a,b' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD append_spaces.
    " Characterize current RIGHT SHIFT behavior, not whitespace trimming.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = '' i_value = ` new ` i_skip_initial = abap_true )
      exp = ` new` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>append_delimited(
        i_current = 'a' i_value = ` b ` i_delimiter = ','
        i_skip_initial = abap_true )
      exp = `a, b` ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD trim_edges.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>trim_delimiters( ';;a;;b;;' )
      exp = 'a;;b' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>trim_delimiters(
        i_value = ',a,' i_delimiter = ',' )
      exp = 'a' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>trim_delimiters( ` ;a; ` )
      exp = ` ;a; ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>trim_delimiters( ';;;' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>trim_delimiters( '' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD alpha_values.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>alpha_output( '000123' ) exp = '123' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>alpha_output( '000' ) exp = '0' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>alpha_output( '00AB01' ) exp = 'AB01' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>alpha_output( '   ' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD alpha_spaces.
    " RIGHT SHIFT preserves length and blocks the subsequent zero stripping.
    DATA(l_value) = zcl_fi_das_utility=>alpha_output( `  000123  ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = l_value exp = `  000123` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = strlen( l_value ) exp = 8 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD boolean_values.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>bool_to_text( CONV #( abap_true ) )
      exp = CONV char5( 'true' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>bool_to_text( CONV #( abap_false ) )
      exp = CONV CHAR5( `false` ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>bool_to_text( 'true' )
      exp = CONV CHAR5( `false` ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD truncate_values.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>truncate_decimal(
        i_value = CONV decfloat34( '12.349' ) i_decimals = 2 )
      exp = CONV decfloat34( '12.34' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>truncate_decimal(
        i_value = CONV decfloat34( '-12.349' ) i_decimals = 2 )
      exp = CONV decfloat34( '-12.34' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD truncate_bounds.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>truncate_decimal(
        i_value = CONV decfloat34( '-1.99' ) i_decimals = 0 )
      exp = CONV decfloat34( -1 ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>truncate_decimal(
        i_value = CONV decfloat34( '1.123456789012349' ) i_decimals = 14 )
      exp = CONV decfloat34( '1.12345678901234' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>truncate_decimal( i_value = 0 i_decimals = 14 ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD truncate_errors.
    DATA lt_decimals TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF i WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_decimals = VALUE #( ( -1 ) ( 15 ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_decimals INTO DATA(l_decimals).
      TRY.
          zcl_fi_das_utility=>truncate_decimal(
            i_value = 1 i_decimals = l_decimals ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( 'Invalid truncation precision accepted' ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
            act = lx_error->if_t100_message~t100key-msgno exp = '018' ).
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD percentage_numbers.
    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ts_case,
        input    TYPE string,
        expected TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_percentage,
      END OF ts_case.
    DATA lt_cases TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ts_case WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_cases = VALUE #(
      ( input = '12.34' expected = '12.3' )
      ( input = '12,35' expected = '12.4' )
      ( input = '+7.5' expected = '7.5' )
      ( input = '-7,5' expected = '-7.5' )
      ( input = ' 1 2 , 5 ' expected = '12.5' )
      ( input = '0' expected = '0' ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_cases INTO DATA(ls_case).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>parse_percentage( ls_case-input )
        exp = ls_case-expected msg = ls_case-input ).
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD percentage_blank.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>parse_percentage( '' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>parse_percentage( '   ' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD percentage_invalid.
    DATA(lt_values) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_utility=>tt_string(
      ( `1,234.5` ) ( `1.2.3` ) ( `abc` ) ( `12%` )
      ( `+` ) ( `.5` ) ( `5.` ) ( `1E2` ) ( `--1` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_values INTO DATA(l_value).
      TRY.
          zcl_fi_das_utility=>parse_percentage( l_value ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( |Invalid percentage accepted: { l_value }| ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
            act = lx_error->if_t100_message~t100key-msgno exp = '012' ).
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD percentage_overflow.
    TRY.
        zcl_fi_das_utility=>parse_percentage( '1000000000000' ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( 'Packed percentage overflow accepted' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          act = lx_error->if_t100_message~t100key-msgno exp = '012' ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_bound( lx_error->previous ).
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD bucket_split.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>month_bucket(
        i_date = '20240514' i_split_day = 15 ) exp = CONV d( '20240501' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>month_bucket(
        i_date = '20240515' i_split_day = 15 ) exp = CONV d( '20240501' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>month_bucket(
        i_date = '20240516' i_split_day = 15 ) exp = CONV d( '20240601' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD bucket_rollover.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>month_bucket(
        i_date = '20241231' i_split_day = 15 ) exp = CONV d( '20250101' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>month_bucket(
        i_date = '20240229' i_split_day = 28 ) exp = CONV d( '20240301' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>month_bucket(
        i_date = '20230228' i_split_day = 27 ) exp = CONV d( '20230301' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD month_differences.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>months_between(
        i_from = '20231231' i_to = '20250101' ) exp = 13 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>months_between(
        i_from = '20250101' i_to = '20231231' ) exp = -13 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>months_between(
        i_from = '20240229' i_to = '20240201' ) exp = 0 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_grouping.
    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ts_case,
        format   TYPE xudcpfm,
        expected TYPE string,
      END OF ts_case.
    DATA lt_cases TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ts_case WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_cases = VALUE #(
      ( format = 'X' expected = '1,234,567.89' )
      ( format = 'Y' expected = '1 234 567,89' )
      ( format = space expected = '1.234.567,89' ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_cases INTO DATA(ls_case).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
          i_amount = CONV #( '1234567.89' ) i_decimal_format = ls_case-format )
        exp = ls_case-expected msg = CONV string( ls_case-format ) ).
    ENDLOOP.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '1491846.48' ) i_decimal_format = 'Y' )
      exp = '1 491 846,48' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '901339.30' ) i_decimal_format = 'Y' )
      exp = '901 339,30' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_no_grouping.
    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ts_case,
        format   TYPE xudcpfm,
        expected TYPE string,
      END OF ts_case.
    DATA lt_cases TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ts_case WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_cases = VALUE #(
      ( format = 'X' expected = '1234567.89' )
      ( format = 'Y' expected = '1234567,89' )
      ( format = space expected = '1234567,89' ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_cases INTO DATA(ls_case).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
          i_amount = CONV #( '1234567.89' ) i_decimal_format = ls_case-format
          i_use_grouping = abap_false )
        exp = ls_case-expected ).
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_padding.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = 1 i_decimal_format = 'X' ) exp = '01.00' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = 1 i_decimal_format = 'X'
        i_pad_nonzero_below_ten = abap_false ) exp = '1.00' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = 0 i_decimal_format = 'X' ) exp = '0.00' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = 10 i_decimal_format = 'X' ) exp = '10.00' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_rounding.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '1234.565' ) i_decimal_format = 'X' )
      exp = '1,234.57' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '999.999' ) i_decimal_format = 'Y' )
      exp = '1 000,00' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_bounds.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '1234.6' ) i_decimal_format = 'X' i_decimals = 0 )
      exp = '1,235' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '12.12345678' ) i_decimal_format = 'X'
        i_decimals = 8 ) exp = '12.12345678' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_errors.
    DATA lt_decimals TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF i WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_decimals = VALUE #( ( -1 ) ( 9 ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_decimals INTO DATA(l_decimals).
      TRY.
          zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
            i_amount = 1 i_decimal_format = 'X' i_decimals = l_decimals ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( 'Invalid amount precision accepted' ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
            act = lx_error->if_t100_message~t100key-msgno exp = '018' ).
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_negative.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '-1234.5' ) i_decimal_format = space )
      exp = '-1.234,50' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = CONV #( '-1.5' ) i_decimal_format = 'X' )
      exp = '-01.50' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD current_day_initial.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>is_current_utc_day( CONV timestampl( 0 ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD timezone_initial.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      zcl_fi_das_utility=>utc_to_cet_timestamp( CONV timestampl( 0 ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltc_parity_helpers IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD setup.
    mo_cut = NEW #( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD null_allowlist.
    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ts_case,
        name  TYPE string,
        token TYPE string,
      END OF ts_case,
      tt_case TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ts_case WITH EMPTY KEY.

    DATA(lt_cases) = VALUE tt_case(
      ( name = 'COSTCENTERDESC' token = '""' )
      ( name = 'DATFM'          token = '""' )
      ( name = 'DECIMAL_FORMAT' token = '""' )
      ( name = 'FULL_NAME'      token = '""' )
      ( name = 'MANAGEUNIT'     token = '""' )
      ( name = 'NOTIF_24_HR'    token = '""' )
      ( name = 'REVIWED_BY'     token = '""' )
      ( name = 'USERNAME'       token = '""' )
      ( name = 'EDGEWTDAMT'     token = '"0.00"' )
      ( name = 'GRVALUE_USD'    token = '"0.00"' )
      ( name = 'INVVALUE_CAL'   token = '"0.00"' )
      ( name = 'INVVALUE_USD'   token = '"0.00"' )
      ( name = 'LASTMONTHWRKCOMP' token = '"0.00"' )
      ( name = 'CHANGEDTIME'    token = '"PT00H00M00S"' ) ).

    LOOP AT lt_cases INTO DATA(ls_case).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        act = mo_cut->is_equivalent_null(
          i_name = ls_case-name i_expected_token = 'null'
          i_actual_token = ls_case-token )
        msg = |Approved NULL mapping for { ls_case-name }| ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
        act = mo_cut->is_equivalent_null(
          i_name = ls_case-name i_expected_token = ls_case-token
          i_actual_token = 'null' )
        msg = |Reverse NULL mapping forbidden for { ls_case-name }| ).
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD null_rejections.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->is_equivalent_null(
        i_name = 'INVVALUE' i_expected_token = 'null'
        i_actual_token = '"0.00"' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->is_equivalent_null(
        i_name = 'AMOUNTACCRUED' i_expected_token = 'null'
        i_actual_token = '"0.00"' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->is_equivalent_null(
        i_name = 'EDGEWTDAMT' i_expected_token = 'null'
        i_actual_token = '"1.00"' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->is_equivalent_null(
        i_name = 'NOTIF_24_HR' i_expected_token = 'null'
        i_actual_token = '"X"' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->is_equivalent_null(
        i_name = 'CHANGEDTIME' i_expected_token = 'null'
        i_actual_token = '"PT01H00M00S"' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD time_padding.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->normalize_odata_time( '"PT9H4M1S"' )
      exp = '"PT09H04M01S"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->normalize_odata_time( '"PT23H59M59S"' )
      exp = '"PT23H59M59S"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->normalize_odata_time( '"PT0H0M0S"' )
      exp = '"PT00H00M00S"' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD time_invalid.
    DATA(lt_tokens) = VALUE stringtab(
      ( `null` ) ( `"PT24H00M00S"` ) ( `"PT00H60M00S"` )
      ( `"PT00H00M60S"` ) ( `"PTaH0M0S"` )
      ( `"PT1H2M"` ) ( `"PT1H2M3Sx"` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_tokens INTO DATA(lv_token).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        act = mo_cut->normalize_odata_time( lv_token )
        exp = lv_token ).
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD missing_property_scope.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      mo_cut->ignore_missing_property( 'ID' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->ignore_missing_property( 'PONUMBER' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->ignore_missing_property( 'INVVALUE' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD aggregate_order.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      mo_cut->equal_aggregate_content(
        i_expected = 'A;B;C' i_actual = 'C;A;B' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD aggregate_multiplicity.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->equal_aggregate_content(
        i_expected = 'A;A;B' i_actual = 'A;B' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->equal_aggregate_content(
        i_expected = 'A;A;B' i_actual = 'A;B;B' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD aggregate_empty_slots.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      mo_cut->equal_aggregate_content(
        i_expected = ';A;B' i_actual = 'B;A;' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->equal_aggregate_content(
        i_expected = 'A;;B' i_actual = 'A;B' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      mo_cut->split_aggregate_empty( `` ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->split_aggregate_empty( ';' )
      exp = VALUE stringtab( ( `` ) ( `` ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD aggregate_field_scope.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      mo_cut->is_unordered_aggregate_field( 'GLACCOUNT' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      mo_cut->is_unordered_aggregate_field( 'SUPPLIERNAME' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD escaped_strings.
    mo_cut->compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'memory' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '00010'
      i_name = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
      i_expected_token = '"S \u0026 K"'
      i_actual_token = '"S & K"' ).
    mo_cut->compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'memory' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '00010'
      i_name = 'CHANGEDTIME'
      i_expected_token = '"PT9H4M1S"'
      i_actual_token = '"PT09H04M01S"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( mo_cut->mt_differences ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD strict_property_diff.
    mo_cut->compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'memory' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '00010'
      i_name = 'INVVALUE'
      i_expected_token = 'null' i_actual_token = '"0.00"' ).
    mo_cut->compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'memory' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '00010'
      i_name = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
      i_expected_token = '"A;B"' i_actual_token = '"B;A"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->mv_difference_count exp = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( mo_cut->mt_differences ) exp = 2 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD empty_results.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      mo_cut->build_expected_index( '{"d":{"results":[]}}' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD nested_json.
    DATA(lv_json) =
      `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"00010",`
      && `"nested":{"text":"brace } and quote \"","items":[1,2]}}]}}`.
    DATA(lt_index) = mo_cut->build_expected_index( lv_json ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lines( lt_index ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      xsdbool( line_exists( lt_index[ ponumber = '1' itemno = '00010' ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->read_result_property(
        i_json = lv_json i_index = 1 i_property = 'ITEMNO' )
      exp = '00010' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD duplicate_keys.
    DATA(lt_index) = mo_cut->build_expected_index(
      `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"00010"},`
      && `{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"00010"}]}}` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lines( lt_index ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->mv_difference_count exp = 1 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD missing_key.
    TRY.
        DATA(lt_index) = mo_cut->build_expected_index(
          '{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1"}]}}' ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( 'Missing ITEMNO must be rejected' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD unclosed_string.
    TRY.
        DATA(lv_end) = mo_cut->read_json_string_end(
          i_text = '"open' i_start = 0 ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( 'Unclosed string must be rejected' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD diagnostic_cap.
    DATA(lv_count) = mo_cut->mc_max_log + 1.
    DO lv_count TIMES.
      mo_cut->add_difference( 'difference' ).
    ENDDO.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->mv_difference_count exp = lv_count ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( mo_cut->mt_differences ) exp = mo_cut->mc_max_log ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltcl_invoice_null_propagation IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD setup.
    CREATE OBJECT mo_cut.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD invoice_zero.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_invoice_value( 0 )
      exp = 0
      msg = 'Invoice value 0 must stay 0' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD invoice_negative.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_invoice_value( -1 )
      exp = 0
      msg = 'Negative invoice value must be normalized to 0' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD invoice_positive.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_invoice_value( CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '12.34' ) )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '12.34' )
      msg = 'Positive invoice value must pass through unchanged' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD service_zero_invoice.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_accrued_amount(
              i_pstype            = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
              i_total_work_amount = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '100.00' )
              i_gr_value          = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '50.00' )
              i_invoice_value     = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '0.00' ) )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '100.00' )
      msg = 'Service item with zero invoice must accrue full work amount' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD material_zero_invoice.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_accrued_amount(
              i_pstype            = 'M'
              i_total_work_amount = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '100.00' )
              i_gr_value          = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '75.00' )
              i_invoice_value     = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '0.00' ) )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '75.00' )
      msg = 'Material item with zero invoice must accrue GR value' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD accrual_clamped_zero.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_accrued_amount(
              i_pstype            = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
              i_total_work_amount = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '100.00' )
              i_gr_value          = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '50.00' )
              i_invoice_value     = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '120.00' ) )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '0.00' )
      msg = 'Accrued amount must never go below 0' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD accrual_difference.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_accrued_amount(
              i_pstype            = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
              i_total_work_amount = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '100.00' )
              i_gr_value          = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '50.00' )
              i_invoice_value     = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '40.00' ) )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '60.00' )
      msg = 'Accrued amount must equal base minus invoice' ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltc_amdp_filters IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD setup.
    mo_cut = NEW #( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD invalid_filter_wraps_error.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    lt_result = VALUE #( ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' ) ).
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME = (` ).

    TRY.
        mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
          EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
          CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( 'Invalid SQL filter must raise a dashboard error' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_bound( lx_error->previous ).
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_cp_case_sensitive_include.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Quality Solutions' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'quantum corp' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME LIKE '%Q%'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 1
      msg = 'A case-sensitive Q pattern matches Quality, not lowercase quantum' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Quality Solutions' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_cp_case_sensitive_exclude.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Quality Solutions' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'quantum corp' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME NOT LIKE '%Q%'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 2
      msg = 'Excluding uppercase Q keeps lowercase quantum and Fuji' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ suppliername = 'quantum corp' ] ) )
      msg = 'Lowercase q is not the uppercase Q excluded by the predicate' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' ] ) )
      msg = 'Fuji has no uppercase Q and remains in the result' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_eq_matches_exact_value.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'fuji corp' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'FUJI' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000004' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Hitachi' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME = 'FUJI'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 1
      msg = 'EQ is exact equality, not case-insensitive or substring matching' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'FUJI' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_cp_and_eq_combined.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Q' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'BioClinica Inc' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'bioclinica small' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000004' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000005' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'quantum' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME = 'Q' OR SUPPLIERNAME LIKE '%Bio%'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 2
      msg = 'Exact Q OR a case-sensitive Bio substring matches two rows' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ suppliername = 'Q' ] ) )
      msg = 'The exact EQ branch matches Q' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ suppliername = 'BioClinica Inc' ] ) )
      msg = 'The CP branch matches BioClinica' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_multiple_cp_filters_or.
    "Testuje: viacerí CP filtre v tej istej property (OR logika)
    "Filter: substringof('Q',SUPPLIERNAME) OR substringof('Bio',SUPPLIERNAME)
    "Očakávaný výsledok: vrátia riadky s 'Q' ALEBO 'Bio'

    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Quality' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'BioClinica' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Quantum' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000004' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME LIKE '%Q%' OR SUPPLIERNAME LIKE '%Bio%'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 3
      msg = 'Uppercase Q matches both Quality and Quantum; Bio adds one more row' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ suppliername = 'Quantum' ] ) )
      msg = 'Quantum begins with uppercase Q and must match the Q pattern' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_cp_empty_pattern.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Quality' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = '' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME LIKE '%'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 2
      msg = 'A percent-only LIKE pattern matches both nonempty and empty names' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD filter_sort_and_page_across.
    DATA lt_package TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    DATA lt_result  TYPE tt_projected_result.

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `POCURRENCY = 'JPY'`
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` ) )
      paging = VALUE #( skip = 1 top = 1 ) ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------
* package 1
*--------------------------------------------------------------------

    lt_package = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001'
        itemno = '00001'
        suppliername = 'Zulu'
        pocurrency = 'USD' ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING
        is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING
        ct_result = lt_package ).

    mo_cut->append_package_to_result(
      CHANGING
        ct_package = lt_package
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------
* package 2
*--------------------------------------------------------------------

    lt_package = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000002'
        itemno = '00001'
        suppliername = 'Bravo'
        pocurrency = 'JPY' )

      ( ponumber = '8000000003'
        itemno = '00001'
        suppliername = 'Charlie'
        pocurrency = 'JPY' )

      ( ponumber = '8000000004'
        itemno = '00001'
        suppliername = 'Delta'
        pocurrency = 'JPY' ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING
        is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING
        ct_result = lt_package ).

    mo_cut->append_package_to_result(
      CHANGING
        ct_package = lt_package
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------
* global sort + paging
*--------------------------------------------------------------------

    DATA(l_total_count) = lines( lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING
        is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING
        is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------
* assertions
*--------------------------------------------------------------------

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = l_total_count
      exp = 3 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 1 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Charlie' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_eq_filters_rows.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' companycode = '2028' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' companycode = '6305' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `COMPANYCODE = '2028'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lines( lt_result ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lt_result[ 1 ]-companycode exp = '2028' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_contains_filters_rows.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001'
        polinedesc = 'GSD Local Support (Jan, 2027)' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001'
        polinedesc = 'Enterprise Plan' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `POLINEDESC LIKE '%Local Support%'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lines( lt_result ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000001' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_two_props_are_anded.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' companycode = '2028' pocurrency = 'JPY' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' companycode = '2028' pocurrency = 'USD' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' companycode = '6305' pocurrency = 'JPY' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `COMPANYCODE = '2028' AND POCURRENCY = 'JPY'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lines( lt_result ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000001' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_same_prop_is_ored.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' pocurrency = 'JPY' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' pocurrency = 'USD' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' pocurrency = 'EUR' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `POCURRENCY = 'JPY' OR POCURRENCY = 'USD'` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lines( lt_result ) exp = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ ponumber = '8000000001' ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ ponumber = '8000000002' ] ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_with_sort_and_paging.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' pocurrency = 'JPY' suppliername = 'Charlie' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' pocurrency = 'JPY' suppliername = 'Alpha' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' pocurrency = 'USD' suppliername = 'Bravo' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `POCURRENCY = 'JPY'`
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` ) )
      paging  = VALUE #( skip = 1 top = 1 ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = lines( lt_result ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Charlie' ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltc_request_processing IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD setup.
    mo_cut = NEW #( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD empty_filter_keeps_rows.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00002'
        suppliername = 'Bravo' original_order = 42 )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001'
        suppliername = 'Alpha' original_order = 17 ) ).
    DATA(lt_expected) = lt_result.
    DATA ls_request TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request.

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result exp = lt_expected
      msg = 'An empty filter preserves all rows, values and original order' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD empty_rows_skip_filter.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `SUPPLIERNAME = (` ).

    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = lt_result
      msg = 'Empty input bypasses AMDP even for invalid SQL' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------
* full-processing decisions
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

  METHOD amdp_po_filter_no_full.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*40102*' ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request )
      msg = 'PO number AMDP filter does not require generic full processing' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amdp_item_filter_no_full.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'ITEMNO' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*00002*' ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request )
      msg = 'Item number AMDP filter does not require generic full processing' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amdp_supplier_filter_no.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*Fuji*' ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request )
      msg = 'Supplier name AMDP filter does not require generic full processing' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD no_filter_no_full_processing.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request( ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request )
      msg = 'No generic filter or orderby leaves full processing unnecessary' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_filter_requires_full.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_string = `COMPANYCODE = '2028'`
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000000001' ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request )
      msg = 'A generic filter still requires full processing when an AMDP filter is present' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD orderby_requires_full.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request )
      msg = 'An orderby requires full processing' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

    " Contains filters

  METHOD po_contains_builds_like.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*40102*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS 'PONUMBER' )
      msg = 'PONUMBER CP is extracted into the AMDP predicate' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '%40102%' )
      msg = 'PONUMBER CP wildcards become a SQL contains pattern' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = ls_request-filter-filter_string
      msg = 'An AMDP-only filter does not require generic filtering' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD item_contains_keeps_zeros.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'ITEMNO' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*2*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-item CS '%2%' )
      msg = 'ITEMNO contains keeps the searched digit without zero-padding' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD mixed_eq_and_contains.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000055868' )
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*7*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '8000055868' )
      msg = 'The EQ alternative is present in the AMDP predicate' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '%7%' )
      msg = 'The CP alternative is present as a contains pattern' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS 'OR' )
      msg = 'EQ and CP alternatives on PONUMBER are ORed' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = ls_request-filter-filter_string ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD supplier_contains_builds_like.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*Fuji*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername CS '%Fuji%' )
      msg = 'Supplier filter preserves the supplied case in the AMDP predicate' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD supplier_pattern_no_wildcards.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*Fuji*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername CS '%Fuji%' )
      msg = 'CP wildcards become SQL wildcards around the original value' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername CS '*Fuji*' )
      msg = 'ABAP CP asterisks are not retained in the SQL predicate' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD supplier_keeps_inner_spaces.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP'
          low = '*Lee Hecht*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername CS '%Lee Hecht%' )
      msg = 'Supplier search preserves the internal space and source case' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD item_contains_digit_two.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'ITEMNO' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*2*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-item CS '%2%' )
      msg = 'Searching digit 2 does not convert it to zero-padded ITEMNO' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amdp_filter_empty_by_default.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request( ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( ls_request-filter_for_amdp ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( ls_request-filter-filter_string ).
  ENDMETHOD.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------
* sort + paging (in place)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

  METHOD sort_ascending_with_paging.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Charlie' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Alpha' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Bravo' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` ) )
      paging  = VALUE #( skip = 1 top = 1 ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 1 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Bravo' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD sort_descending.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Alpha' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Bravo' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME`
                          descending = abap_true ) ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Bravo' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD explicit_po_desc_is_preserved.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00002' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Same' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` )
                         ( property = `PONUMBER` descending = abap_true )
                         ( property = `PONUMBER` descending = abap_false ) ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000002'
      msg = 'An explicit descending PO sorter is retained instead of being overridden' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 2 ]-itemno
      exp = '00001'
      msg = 'The missing item tie-breaker remains ascending' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD sort_unknown_property_no_dump.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `NOT_A_COLUMN` ) ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000002'
      msg = 'An unknown sort component is ignored without changing input order' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD top_zero_requested_empty.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      paging = VALUE #( top = 0 top_requested = abap_true ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = lt_result
      msg = 'Explicit $top=0 must empty the page result' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD top_zero_not_requested_all.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      paging = VALUE #( top = 0 ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 2
      msg = 'An unrequested top=0 keeps the unpaged result set' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD paging_window_boundaries.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    DO 10 TIMES.
      APPEND VALUE #( ponumber = |80000000{ sy-index WIDTH = 2
                                            PAD = '0' ALIGN = RIGHT }|
                      itemno   = '00001' ) TO lt_result.
    ENDDO.

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `PONUMBER` ) )
      paging  = VALUE #( skip = 8 top = 5 ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 2
      msg = 'Skip 8 and top 5 on ten sorted rows returns the final two rows' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000009' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD no_orderby_leaves_table_asis.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      paging = VALUE #( skip = 0 top = 0 ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000002'
      msg = 'Without orderby, row order is left untouched' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_sort_gets_stable_ties.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00002' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00002' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Same' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` ) ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000001' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-itemno
      exp = '00001' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 2 ]-itemno
      exp = '00002' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 3 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000002' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD package_mapping_preserves_ord.
    DATA lt_package TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    DATA lt_result  TYPE tt_projected_result.

    lt_package = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Charlie' pocurrency = 'USD' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Bravo'   pocurrency = 'JPY' ) ).

    mo_cut->append_package_to_result(
      CHANGING
        ct_package = lt_package
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( lt_package ).

    lt_package = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Alpha' pocurrency = 'EUR' ) ).

    mo_cut->append_package_to_result(
      CHANGING
        ct_package = lt_package
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 3 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Charlie' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 2 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Bravo' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 3 ]-suppliername
      exp = 'Alpha' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD empty_package_is_ignored.
    DATA lt_package TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    DATA lt_result  TYPE tt_projected_result.

    mo_cut->append_package_to_result(
      CHANGING
        ct_package = lt_package
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( lt_result ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD multiple_packages_keep_ord.
    DATA lt_package TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    DATA lt_result  TYPE tt_projected_result.

    lt_package = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '1' )
      ( ponumber = '2' ) ).

    mo_cut->append_package_to_result(
      CHANGING
        ct_package = lt_package
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

    lt_package = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '3' )
      ( ponumber = '4' ) ).

    mo_cut->append_package_to_result(
      CHANGING
        ct_package = lt_package
        ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp = '1' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 2 ]-ponumber
      exp = '2' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 3 ]-ponumber
      exp = '3' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 4 ]-ponumber
      exp = '4' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD pstype_desc_moved_for_under.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      mode    = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `PSTYPE` descending = abap_true ) ) ).

    mo_cut->remove_default_sorting( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = ls_request-orderby
      msg = 'The UI default PSTYPE-descending order is moved to the AMDP key selection' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = ls_request-pstype_desc_requested
      msg = 'PSTYPE-descending is flagged for the AMDP key selection' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request )
      msg = 'Removing default sorting avoids generic full processing' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD pstype_desc_moved_for_over.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      mode    = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD po_item_prefix_removed.

  METHOD pstype_sort_kept_for_exclude.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' pstype = '0' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' pstype = '0' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `PSTYPE`
                           descending = abap_true ) ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-pstype
      exp = '9'
      msg = 'Service rows sort before material on descending PSTYPE' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 2 ]-ponumber
      exp = '8000000001'
      msg = 'Default ascending PO tie-breaker remains within PSTYPE' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD pstype_sort_desc_mixed_rows.
    DATA lt_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.

    lt_result = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' pstype = '0' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' ) ).

    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `PSTYPE`
                           descending = abap_true ) )
      paging  = VALUE #( skip = 2 top = 1 ) ).

    mo_cut->apply_sort(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    mo_cut->apply_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request
      CHANGING  ct_result  = lt_result ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_result )
      exp = 1 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_result[ 1 ]-pstype
      exp = '0' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD review_day_boundary.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_review_action(
              i_changed_date = '20260906'
              i_review_day   = 7
              i_current_date = '20260906' )
      exp = 'K'
      msg = 'Day 6 before the review boundary must keep the reviewed status' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_review_action(
              i_changed_date = '20260906'
              i_review_day   = 7
              i_current_date = '20260907' )
      exp = 'R'
      msg = 'Crossing from day 6 to day 7 must reset the reviewed status' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_review_action(
              i_changed_date = '20260907'
              i_review_day   = 7
              i_current_date = '20260907' )
      exp = 'K'
      msg = 'A review performed on the review day must stay reviewed on that day' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD po_multiple_eq_same_property.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000401022' )
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000000001' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '8000401022' ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '8000000001' ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS 'OR' )
      msg = 'Multiple included EQ values for one property are OR alternatives' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( ls_request-filter-filter_string ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD po_eq_and_cp_same_property.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000055868' )
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*7*' ) ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '8000055868' )
      msg = 'The EQ value is extracted to the PONUMBER AMDP predicate' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '%7%' )
      msg = 'The CP value becomes a LIKE contains pattern' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS 'OR' )
      msg = 'EQ and CP on PONUMBER are combined as OR alternatives' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( ls_request-filter-filter_string ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD supplier_multiple_cp_same_prop.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*Fuji*' )

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername CS 'OR' )
      msg = 'Multiple CP options on SUPPLIERNAME are OR alternatives' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD item_multiple_eq_same_property.

  METHOD empty_contains_pattern.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '' ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      act = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber
      msg = 'An empty CP range is still forwarded as an AMDP filter' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = ls_request-filter-filter_string
      msg = 'An AMDP-only empty CP range does not leave generic filtering active' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD item_number_with_leading_zeros.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD exclude_sign_filters.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD unsupported_option_generic.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD supplier_filter_with_case_sens.

  ENDMETHOD.
