  ENDMETHOD.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------
* AMDP FILTER EXTRACTION COVERAGE
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

  METHOD amdp_filter_mixed_properties.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter = VALUE #(
        filter_select_options = VALUE #(
          ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000000001' )
          ( property = 'COMPANYCODE' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '2028' )
          ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*Fuji*' )
        )
        filter_string = `COMPANYCODE = '2028'` ) ).

    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber CS '8000000001' )
      msg = 'PONUMBER is extracted into its AMDP predicate' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername CS '%Fuji%' )
      msg = 'SUPPLIERNAME is extracted into its AMDP predicate' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ls_request-filter-filter_string
      exp = `COMPANYCODE = '2028'`
      msg = 'The generic property condition remains available for generic filtering' ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------
* Scope NULL / wildcard matrix: 5 scenarios x 62 scopes.
* Users xsa-null-01..62@takeda.com must exist in ZFI_DAS role table
* (ABAP) and in XSA with identical COMPANY_CODE / MANAGEMENT_UNIT /
* COST_CENTER. Fixtures: xsa-scope-<scenario>-NN.json, no owner.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------

CLASS lcl_scope_parity DEFINITION FINAL FOR TESTING
  DURATION LONG
  RISK LEVEL HARMLESS.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    CLASS-METHODS run_case
      IMPORTING
        i_scenario TYPE string
        i_number   TYPE numc2
      RAISING
        cx_static_check.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS lcl_scope_parity IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD run_case.
    DATA lo_parity TYPE REF TO ltc_fi_das_gateway_parity.
    DATA ls_case TYPE ltc_fi_das_gateway_parity=>ts_case.

    ls_case-fixture = |xsa-scope-{ i_scenario }-{ i_number }.json|.
    ls_case-email   = |xsa-null-{ i_number }@takeda.com|.

    CASE i_scenario.
      WHEN 'over-service' OR 'over-material'.
        ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
      WHEN 'under-service' OR 'under-material'.
        ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
      WHEN 'exclude'.
        ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
      WHEN OTHERS.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( msg = |Unknown scope scenario: { i_scenario }| ).
        RETURN.
    ENDCASE.

    CASE i_scenario.
      WHEN 'over-service' OR 'under-service'.
        ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
      WHEN 'over-material' OR 'under-material'.
        ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_material.
    ENDCASE.

    CREATE OBJECT lo_parity.
    lo_parity->run_paged_case( is_case = ls_case ).
    lo_parity->assert_no_differences( ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltc_scope_over_service DEFINITION FINAL FOR TESTING
  DURATION LONG
  RISK LEVEL HARMLESS.
  PRIVATE SECTION.
    METHODS run_scope_case
      IMPORTING i_number TYPE numc2
      RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_01 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_02 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_03 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_04 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_05 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_06 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_07 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_08 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_09 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_10 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.
    METHODS test_scope_11 FOR TESTING RAISING cx_static_check.

CLASS ltc_fi_das_gateway_parity IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD setup.
    CLEAR:
      mt_differences,
      mv_difference_count.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_over_material.
    DATA ls_case TYPE ts_case.

    ls_case-fixture =

  METHOD test_inlinecount_with_top_one.
    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    ls_case-top = 1.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 1
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_page_1 )
      exp = 1
      msg = '$top=1 must return exactly one row on the first page' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_count_1 > 1 )
      msg = '__count must represent the total filtered set, not the page size' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count_2
      exp = lv_count_1
      msg = '__count must stay stable across pages' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 )
      exp = 1
      msg = 'The second top=1 page must still contain one row when total count > 1' ).

    assert_no_duplica_across_pages(
      i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1
      i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).

    assert_row_keys_match(
      i_json_left      = lv_json_page_1
      i_left_index     = 1
      i_json_right     = lv_json_page_2
      i_right_index    = 1
      i_expected_match = abap_false ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_no_inlinecount.
    DATA:
      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    ls_case-top = 1.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    assert_inlinecount_absent(
      i_json = lv_json ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count
      exp = 0
      msg = 'Missing $inlinecount must not produce a parsed count value' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json )
      exp = 1 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_skip_beyond_result_set.
    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    ls_case-top = 1.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 999999
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 )
      exp = 0
      msg = '$skip beyond the result set must return an empty results array' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count_2
      exp = lv_count_1
      msg = '__count must remain valid even when the page is empty' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_top_zero.
    DATA:
      ls_case_full  TYPE ts_case,
      ls_case_zero  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_full  TYPE string,
      lv_json_zero  TYPE string,
      lv_count_full TYPE int8,
      lv_count_zero TYPE int8.

    ls_case_full-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case_full-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case_full-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    ls_case_full-top = 1.
    ls_case_full-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case_full-inlinecount = abap_true.

    ls_case_zero = ls_case_full.
    ls_case_zero-top = 0.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_full
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_full
        e_inline_count = lv_count_full ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_zero
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_zero
        e_inline_count = lv_count_zero ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_zero )
      exp = 0
      msg = '$top=0 must not return any result rows' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count_zero
      exp = lv_count_full
      msg = '$top=0 must preserve the same total inline count' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_po_value_help.
    DATA:
      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8,
      lv_rows  TYPE i.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-select_list = 'PONUMBER,POLINEDESC'.
    ls_case-filter_raw = `substringof('40102',PONUMBER)`.
    ls_case-top = 50.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    lv_rows = get_result_count( lv_json ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_rows > 0 )
      msg = 'The PO value-help request must return at least one matching row' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_count >= lv_rows )
      msg = 'Inline count must be at least the number of returned rows' ).

    assert_all_rows_match_filter(
      i_json     = lv_json
      i_property = 'PONUMBER'
      i_value    = '40102'
      i_contains = abap_true ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_filter_sort_and_paging.
    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-filter_raw = `POCURRENCY eq 'JPY'`.
    ls_case-orderby = 'SUPPLIERNAME'.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_count_1 >= get_result_count( lv_json_page_1 ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count_2
      exp = lv_count_1
      msg = 'Inline count must stay stable across filtered pages' ).

    assert_all_rows_match_filter(
      i_json     = lv_json_page_1
      i_property = 'POCURRENCY'
      i_value    = 'JPY' ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_page_1
      i_property   = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
      i_descending = abap_false ).

    IF lv_count_1 > 100.
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        act = xsdbool( get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 ) > 0 )
        msg = 'A filtered result count above one page must yield rows on page two' ).
    ENDIF.

    IF get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 ) > 0.
      assert_all_rows_match_filter(
        i_json     = lv_json_page_2
        i_property = 'POCURRENCY'
        i_value    = 'JPY' ).

      assert_rows_sorted(
        i_json       = lv_json_page_2
        i_property   = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
        i_descending = abap_false ).

      assert_no_duplica_across_pages(
        i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1
        i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_under_paging.
    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_page_1 )
      exp = 100
      msg = 'The first Under page must honor $top=100' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 )
      exp = 100
      msg = 'The second Under page must honor $top=100' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count_2
      exp = lv_count_1
      msg = 'Under inline count must stay stable across pages' ).

    assert_no_duplica_across_pages(
      i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1
      i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).

    assert_row_keys_match(
      i_json_left      = lv_json_page_1
      i_left_index     = 100
      i_json_right     = lv_json_page_2
      i_right_index    = 1
      i_expected_match = abap_false ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_excl_paging.
    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_page_1 )
      exp = 100
      msg = 'The first Exclusion page must honor $top=100' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 )
      exp = 100
      msg = 'The second Exclusion page must honor $top=100' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count_2
      exp = lv_count_1
      msg = 'Exclusion inline count must stay stable across pages' ).

    assert_no_duplica_across_pages(
      i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1
      i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).

    assert_row_keys_match(
      i_json_left      = lv_json_page_1
      i_left_index     = 100
      i_json_right     = lv_json_page_2
      i_right_index    = 1
      i_expected_match = abap_false ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_excl_pstype_pages.
    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_desc_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_desc_2 TYPE string,
      lv_json_asc_1  TYPE string,
      lv_json_asc_2  TYPE string.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all.
    ls_case-orderby = 'PSTYPE'.
    ls_case-orderby_desc = abap_true.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_desc_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_desc_2 ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_desc_1
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_true ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_desc_2
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_true ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool(
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_desc_1
                  i_index    = get_result_count( lv_json_desc_1 )
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) )
        >=
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_desc_2
                  i_index    = 1
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) ) )
      msg = 'Descending PSTYPE order must stay correct across the page boundary' ).

    CLEAR ls_case-orderby_desc.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_asc_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_asc_2 ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_asc_1
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_false ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_asc_2
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_false ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool(
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_asc_1
                  i_index    = get_result_count( lv_json_asc_1 )
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) )
        <=
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_asc_2
                  i_index    = 1
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) ) )
      msg = 'Ascending PSTYPE order must stay correct across the page boundary' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_orderby_invalid.
    DATA ls_case TYPE ts_case.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-orderby = 'NOT_A_COLUMN'.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.

    DATA(lv_path) =
      build_resource_path(
        is_case = ls_case
        i_skip  = 0 ).

    execute_gateway_raw(
      EXPORTING
        i_resource_path = lv_path
      IMPORTING
        e_status_code   = DATA(lv_status_code)
        e_status_text   = DATA(lv_status_text)
        e_json          = DATA(lv_json) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_status_code
      exp = 400
      msg = 'Unknown $orderby must be rejected with HTTP 400' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_json CS '"error"' )
      msg = 'Unknown $orderby must return a well-formed OData error body' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = xsdbool( to_upper( lv_json ) CS 'SHORT DUMP' )
      msg = 'Unknown $orderby must not serialize an ABAP short dump' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_status_text IS NOT INITIAL )
      msg = 'The raw Gateway helper must expose the HTTP status text' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_nonexistent_supplier.

      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

        e_json         = lv_json

      exp = 0 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count
      exp = 0
      msg = 'A non-existent supplier must produce __count=0' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_filter_po_or_supplier.
    DATA:
      ls_case      TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json      TYPE string,
      lv_count     TYPE int8,
      lv_row_count TYPE i,
      lv_index     TYPE i.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-filter_raw =
      `(PONUMBER eq '8002077142' or PONUMBER eq '8002077289') `
      && `and substringof('Hitachi',SUPPLIERNAME)`.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    lv_row_count = get_result_count( lv_json ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_row_count > 0 )
      msg = 'Combined OR/AND filter must return at least one live row' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_count >= lv_row_count ) ).

    assert_all_rows_match_filter(
      i_json     = lv_json
      i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
      i_value    = 'Hitachi'
      i_contains = abap_true ).

    WHILE lv_index < lv_row_count.
      lv_index = lv_index + 1.

      DATA(lv_ponumber) =
        read_result_property(
          i_json     = lv_json
          i_index    = lv_index
          i_property = 'PONUMBER' ).

      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        act = xsdbool(
          lv_ponumber = '8002077142'
          OR lv_ponumber = '8002077289' )
        msg = |Row { lv_index } must belong to one of the requested PO numbers| ).
    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_select_calc_status.
    DATA:
      ls_case_select TYPE ts_case,
      ls_case_full   TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_select TYPE string,
      lv_json_full   TYPE string,
      lv_status_sel  TYPE string,
      lv_status_full TYPE string,
      lv_ponumber    TYPE string,
      lv_itemno      TYPE string.

    ls_case_select-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case_select-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case_select-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case_select-select_list = 'PONUMBER,ITEMNO,STATUS'.
    ls_case_select-top = 1.
    ls_case_select-top_specified = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = ls_case_select
      IMPORTING
        e_json  = lv_json_select ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_select )
      exp = 1
      msg = 'Calculated select request must return exactly one row' ).

    read_first_result_key(
      EXPORTING
        i_json     = lv_json_select
      IMPORTING
        e_ponumber = lv_ponumber
        e_itemno   = lv_itemno ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      act = lv_ponumber
      msg = 'Selected response must contain PONUMBER' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      act = lv_itemno
      msg = 'Selected response must contain ITEMNO' ).

    lv_status_sel =
      read_first_result_property(
        i_json     = lv_json_select
        i_property = 'STATUS' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool(
        lv_status_sel IS NOT INITIAL
        AND to_upper( lv_status_sel ) <> 'NULL' )
      msg = 'Calculated STATUS must be present in restricted $select' ).

    ls_case_full = ls_case_select.
    CLEAR:
      ls_case_full-select_list,
      ls_case_full-top,
      ls_case_full-top_specified.
    ls_case_full-filter_raw =
      |PONUMBER eq '{ lv_ponumber }' and ITEMNO eq '{ lv_itemno }'|.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = ls_case_full
      IMPORTING
        e_json  = lv_json_full ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_full )
      exp = 1
      msg = |Full request for { lv_ponumber }/{ lv_itemno } must return one row| ).

    lv_status_full =
      read_first_result_property(
        i_json     = lv_json_full
        i_property = 'STATUS' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_status_sel
      exp = lv_status_full
      msg = |Restricted $select returned a different STATUS for |
         && |{ lv_ponumber }/{ lv_itemno }| ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_skip_high_sorted.
    DATA:
      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-orderby = 'SUPPLIERNAME'.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 999999
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json )
      exp = 0
      msg = 'A high skip on a sorted request must return an empty result page' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_count > 0 )
      msg = 'The inline count must still expose the real filtered result size' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_empty_contains_po.
    DATA:
      ls_case_base   TYPE ts_case,
      ls_case_empty  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_base   TYPE string,
      lv_json_empty  TYPE string,
      lv_count_base  TYPE int8,
      lv_count_empty TYPE int8,
      lt_base_index  TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lt_empty_index TYPE tt_expected_index.

    ls_case_base-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case_base-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case_base-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case_base-top = 10.
    ls_case_base-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case_base-inlinecount = abap_true.

    ls_case_empty = ls_case_base.
    ls_case_empty-filter_raw = `substringof('',PONUMBER)`.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_base
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_base
        e_inline_count = lv_count_base ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_empty
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_empty
        e_inline_count = lv_count_empty ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_count_empty
      exp = lv_count_base
      msg = 'Empty substring filter must keep the same inline count as no filter' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = get_result_count( lv_json_empty )
      exp = get_result_count( lv_json_base )
      msg = 'Empty substring filter must keep the same page size as no filter' ).

    lt_base_index =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = lv_json_base ).

    lt_empty_index =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = lv_json_empty ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lines( lt_empty_index )
      exp = lines( lt_base_index )
      msg = 'Empty substring filter must keep the same row-key set size' ).

    LOOP AT lt_empty_index ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_empty_row>).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        act = xsdbool(
          line_exists(
            lt_base_index[
              ponumber = <ls_empty_row>-ponumber
              itemno   = <ls_empty_row>-itemno ] ) )
        msg = 'Empty substring filter must not change the returned row keys' ).
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_under_po_help.
    DATA:
      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-select_list = 'PONUMBER,POLINEDESC'.
    ls_case-filter_raw = `substringof('89065',PONUMBER)`.
    ls_case-top = 50.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( get_result_count( lv_json ) > 0 )
      msg = 'Under PO value help must return at least one live row' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      act = xsdbool( lv_count >= get_result_count( lv_json ) ) ).

    assert_all_rows_match_filter(
      i_json     = lv_json
      i_property = 'PONUMBER'
      i_value    = '89065'
      i_contains = abap_true ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_under_supplier_help.
    DATA:
      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-select_list = 'SUPPLIERID,SUPPLIERNAME'.
    ls_case-filter_raw = `substringof('Lee Hecht',SUPPLIERNAME)`.
    ls_case-top = 50.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(

      IMPORTING

        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

      msg = 'Under supplier value help must return at least one live row' ).

      act = xsdbool( lv_count >= get_result_count( lv_json ) ) ).

    assert_all_rows_match_filter(
      i_json     = lv_json
      i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
      i_value    = 'Lee Hecht'
      i_contains = abap_true ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD run_paged_case.
    DATA:
      lv_expected_json  TYPE string,
      lv_actual_json    TYPE string,
      lt_expected_index TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lt_actual_index   TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lv_skip           TYPE i,
      lv_page_rows      TYPE i,
      lv_total_count    TYPE int8,
      lv_finished       TYPE abap_bool.

    lv_expected_json =
      read_mime_fixture(
        i_fixture = is_case-fixture ).

    lt_expected_index =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = lv_expected_json ).

    WHILE lv_finished = abap_false.

      CLEAR:
        lv_actual_json,
        lv_total_count,
        lv_page_rows.

      fetch_gateway_page(
        EXPORTING
          is_case       = is_case
          i_skip        = lv_skip
        IMPORTING
          e_json        = lv_actual_json
          e_total_count = lv_total_count ).

      lt_actual_index =
        build_expected_index(
          i_json = lv_actual_json ).

      lv_page_rows = lines( lt_actual_index ).

      compare_actual_page(
        EXPORTING
          i_fixture       = is_case-fixture
          i_expected_json = lv_expected_json
          i_actual_json   = lv_actual_json
          it_actual_index = lt_actual_index
        CHANGING
          ct_expected_index = lt_expected_index ).

      IF  lv_total_count <> lines( lt_expected_index ).
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Inline count differs; Fixture={ is_case-fixture }; |
            && |XSA={ lines( lt_expected_index ) }; |
            && |SEGW={ lv_total_count }| ).
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_page_rows = 0.
        lv_finished = abap_true.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      lv_skip = lv_skip + lv_page_rows.

      IF lv_total_count > 0.
        IF lv_skip >= lv_total_count.
          lv_finished = abap_true.
        ENDIF.
      ELSEIF lv_page_rows < mc_page_size.
        lv_finished = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

      FREE lt_actual_index.

    ENDWHILE.

    LOOP AT lt_expected_index
      ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_expected>)
      WHERE matched = abap_false.

      add_difference(
        i_text =
            |Missing SEGW raw JSON row; Fixture={ is_case-fixture }; |
          && |PO={ <ls_expected>-ponumber }; |
          && |ITEMNO={ <ls_expected>-itemno }| ).

    ENDLOOP.

    FREE:
      lt_expected_index,
      lt_actual_index,
      lv_expected_json,
      lv_actual_json.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD fetch_gateway_page.
    CLEAR:
      e_json,
      e_total_count.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = is_case
        i_skip         = i_skip
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = e_json
        e_inline_count = e_total_count ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD get_gateway_response.
    DATA lv_path TYPE string.

    lv_path =
      build_resource_path(
        is_case = is_case
        i_skip  = i_skip ).

    e_json =
      execute_gateway_get(
        i_resource_path = lv_path ).

    IF is_case-inlinecount = abap_true
       OR is_case-fixture IS NOT INITIAL.

      e_inline_count =
        read_inline_count(
          i_json = e_json ).

    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD build_resource_path.
    DATA:
      lv_email             TYPE string,
      lv_poowner           TYPE string,
      lv_pstype            TYPE string,
      lv_top               TYPE i,
      lv_orderby           TYPE string,
      lv_orderby_upper     TYPE string,
      lv_orderby_direction TYPE string.

    lv_email = odata_quote( i_value = is_case-email ).
    lv_poowner = odata_quote( i_value = is_case-poowner ).
    lv_pstype = odata_quote( i_value = is_case-pstype ).
    lv_top = mc_page_size.

    IF is_case-top_specified = abap_true.
      lv_top = is_case-top.
    ELSEIF is_case-top > 0.
      lv_top = is_case-top.
    ENDIF.

    CASE is_case-flow.
      WHEN zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
        rv_path =
            '/sap/opu/odata/sap/ZFI_DAS_SRV/'
          && |openPOParameters(IP_PSTYPE='{ lv_pstype }',|
          && |IP_EMAIL='{ lv_email }',|
          && |IP_POOWNER='{ lv_poowner }')/Results|.

      WHEN zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
        rv_path =
            '/sap/opu/odata/sap/ZFI_DAS_SRV/'
          && |thresholdParameters(IP_PSTYPE='{ lv_pstype }',|
          && |IP_EMAIL='{ lv_email }',|
          && |IP_POOWNER='{ lv_poowner }')/Results|.

      WHEN zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
        rv_path =
            '/sap/opu/odata/sap/ZFI_DAS_SRV/'
          && |exclusionPOParameters(IP_EMAIL='{ lv_email }',|
          && |IP_POOWNER='{ lv_poowner }')/Results|.
    ENDCASE.

    rv_path =
        rv_path
      && |?$format=json|.

    IF is_case-inlinecount = abap_true
       OR is_case-fixture IS NOT INITIAL.
      rv_path = rv_path && |&$inlinecount=allpages|.
    ENDIF.

    IF ( is_case-top_specified = abap_true AND is_case-top > 0 )
       OR ( is_case-top_specified = abap_true
            AND is_case-top = 0
            AND is_case-fixture IS INITIAL )
       OR is_case-fixture IS NOT INITIAL.
      rv_path = rv_path && |&$top={ lv_top }|.
    ENDIF.

    rv_path = rv_path && |&$skip={ i_skip }|.

    IF is_case-orderby IS NOT INITIAL.

      lv_orderby = trim_text( is_case-orderby ).
      lv_orderby_upper = to_upper( trim_text( lv_orderby ) ).

      IF is_case-orderby_desc = abap_true
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* DESC'
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* DESCENDING'
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* ASC'
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* ASCENDING'.
        lv_orderby_direction = ` desc`.
      ENDIF.

      lv_orderby = lv_orderby && lv_orderby_direction.

      cl_http_utility=>escape_url(
        EXPORTING
          unescaped = lv_orderby
        RECEIVING
          escaped   = lv_orderby ).

      rv_path = rv_path && |&$orderby={ lv_orderby }|.

    ENDIF.

    IF is_case-select_list IS NOT INITIAL.
      rv_path = rv_path && |&$select={ is_case-select_list }|.
    ENDIF.

    append_filter(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = is_case
      CHANGING
        cv_path = rv_path ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD append_filter.
    IF is_case-filter_raw IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    DATA(lv_filter) = is_case-filter_raw.

    cl_http_utility=>escape_url(
      EXPORTING
        unescaped = lv_filter
      RECEIVING
        escaped   = lv_filter ).

    cv_path = cv_path && |&$filter={ lv_filter }|.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD odata_quote.
    rv_value = CONV string( i_value ).
    SHIFT rv_value RIGHT DELETING TRAILING space.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ''''
      IN rv_value
      WITH ''''''.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD execute_gateway_get.
    DATA:
      lv_status_code TYPE i,
      lv_status_text TYPE string.

    execute_gateway_raw(
      EXPORTING
        i_resource_path = i_resource_path
      IMPORTING
        e_status_code   = lv_status_code
        e_status_text   = lv_status_text
        e_json          = rv_json ).

    IF lv_status_code < 200 OR lv_status_code >= 300.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
        TYPE 'E'
        NUMBER '000'
        WITH
            |SEGW returned HTTP { lv_status_code } { lv_status_text }; |
            && |URI={ i_resource_path }; Body={ rv_json }|.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD execute_gateway_raw.
    DATA:
      lo_client          TYPE REF TO /iwfnd/cl_sutil_client_proxy,
      lt_request_header  TYPE /iwfnd/sutil_property_t,
      lt_response_header TYPE /iwfnd/sutil_property_t,
      lv_response_body   TYPE xstring.

    lt_request_header = VALUE #(
      ( name  = '~request_method'
        value = 'GET' )
      ( name  = '~request_uri'
        value = i_resource_path )
      ( name  = 'accept'
        value = 'application/json' ) ).

    lo_client =
      /iwfnd/cl_sutil_client_proxy=>get_instance( ).

    lo_client->web_request(
      EXPORTING
        it_request_header  = lt_request_header
      IMPORTING
        ev_status_code     = e_status_code
        ev_status_text     = e_status_text
        et_response_header = lt_response_header
        ev_response_body   = lv_response_body ).

    e_json =
      /iwfnd/cl_sutil_xml_helper=>transform_to_string(
        lv_response_body ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_inline_count.
    DATA:
      lv_offset TYPE i,
      lv_start  TYPE i,
      lv_end    TYPE i,
      lv_length TYPE i,
      lv_token  TYPE string.

    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF '"__count"'
      IN i_json
      MATCH OFFSET lv_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_length = strlen( i_json ).
    lv_start = lv_offset + 9.

    WHILE lv_start < lv_length
      AND i_json+lv_start(1) <> ':'.
      lv_start = lv_start + 1.
    ENDWHILE.

    lv_start = skip_whitespace(
      i_text     = i_json
      i_position = lv_start + 1 ).

    TRY.
        lv_end = read_json_value_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_start ).
      CATCH cx_root.

    ENDTRY.

    lv_token = substring(
      val = i_json
      off = lv_start
      len = lv_end - lv_start ).

    lv_token = unquote_json(
      i_token = lv_token ).

    IF lv_token IS NOT INITIAL.
      rv_count = CONV int8( lv_token ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD get_result_count.
    DATA:
      lv_array_from TYPE i,
      lv_array_to   TYPE i,
      lv_position   TYPE i,
      lv_value_end  TYPE i,
      lv_char       TYPE char1.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_position = lv_array_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_array_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= lv_array_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char = i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.
        lv_position = lv_position + 1.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      lv_value_end =
        read_json_value_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_position ).

      rv_count = rv_count + 1.
      lv_position = lv_value_end.

    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_inlinecount_absent.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      act = xsdbool( i_json CS '"__count"' )
      msg = 'Response must not contain __count when $inlinecount was not requested' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_all_rows_match_filter.
    DATA:
      lv_row_count   TYPE i,
      lv_row_index   TYPE i,
      lv_object_from TYPE i,
      lv_object_to   TYPE i,
      lv_actual      TYPE string,
      lv_expected    TYPE string.

    lv_row_count =
      get_result_count( i_json ).

    lv_expected = to_upper( i_value ).

    WHILE lv_row_index < lv_row_count.

      lv_row_index = lv_row_index + 1.

      find_result_object_bounds(
        EXPORTING
          i_json        = i_json
          i_index       = lv_row_index
        IMPORTING
          e_object_from = lv_object_from
          e_object_to   = lv_object_to ).

      DATA(lt_properties) =
        read_object_properties(
          i_json        = i_json
          i_object_from = lv_object_from
          i_object_to   = lv_object_to ).

      READ TABLE lt_properties ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_property>)
        WITH TABLE KEY name = i_property.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
          msg =
            |Response row { lv_row_index } does not contain property { i_property }| ).
      ENDIF.

      lv_actual =
        to_upper(
          unquote_json(
            i_token = <ls_property>-token ) ).

      IF i_contains = abap_true.

        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
          act = xsdbool( lv_actual CS lv_expected )
          msg =
            |Row { lv_row_index } property { i_property } must contain { i_value }| ).

      ELSE.

        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          act = lv_actual
          exp = lv_expected
          msg =
            |Row { lv_row_index } property { i_property } must equal { i_value }| ).

      ENDIF.

    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_rows_sorted.
    DATA:
      lv_row_count  TYPE i,
      lv_row_index  TYPE i,
      lv_previous   TYPE string,
      lv_current    TYPE string,
      lv_previous_i TYPE i,
      lv_current_i  TYPE i,
      lv_previous_n TYPE decfloat34,
      lv_current_n  TYPE decfloat34.

    IF i_property <> 'PSTYPE'
       AND i_property <> 'SUPPLIERNAME'
       AND i_property <> 'AMOUNTACCRUED'
       AND i_property <> 'REVIWED_BY'.

      cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
        msg = |assert_rows_sorted does not support { i_property }| ).

    ENDIF.

    lv_row_count =
      get_result_count( i_json ).

    WHILE lv_row_index < lv_row_count.

      lv_row_index = lv_row_index + 1.

      DATA(lv_token) =
        read_result_property(
          i_json     = i_json
          i_index    = lv_row_index
          i_property = i_property ).

      lv_current =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_token ).

      IF to_upper( lv_current ) = 'NULL'.
        CLEAR lv_current.
      ENDIF.

      " JSON strings do not contain the fixed-length ABAP trailing
      " spaces, but remove surrounding whitespace consistently.
      lv_current = trim_text( lv_current ).

      IF lv_row_index > 1.

        IF i_property = 'PSTYPE'.

          lv_previous_i =
            COND i(
              WHEN lv_previous IS INITIAL
              THEN 0
              ELSE CONV i( lv_previous ) ).

          lv_current_i =
            COND i(
              WHEN lv_current IS INITIAL
              THEN 0
              ELSE CONV i( lv_current ) ).

          IF i_descending = abap_true.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              act = xsdbool( lv_previous_i >= lv_current_i )
              msg =
                |PSTYPE descending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
                && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ELSE.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              act = xsdbool( lv_previous_i <= lv_current_i )
              msg =
                |PSTYPE ascending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
                && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF i_property = 'AMOUNTACCRUED'.

          lv_previous_n =
            COND decfloat34(
              WHEN lv_previous IS INITIAL
              THEN 0
              ELSE CONV decfloat34( lv_previous ) ).

          lv_current_n =
            COND decfloat34(
              WHEN lv_current IS INITIAL
              THEN 0
              ELSE CONV decfloat34( lv_current ) ).

          IF i_descending = abap_true.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              act = xsdbool( lv_previous_n >= lv_current_n )
              msg =
                |AMOUNTACCRUED descending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
                && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ELSE.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              act = xsdbool( lv_previous_n <= lv_current_n )
              msg =
                |AMOUNTACCRUED ascending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
                && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ENDIF.

        ELSE.

          " Do not uppercase: production ABAP SORT uses the original
          " character values. The assertion must use the same values.
          IF i_descending = abap_true.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              act = xsdbool( lv_previous >= lv_current )
              msg =
                |{ i_property } descending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
                && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ELSE.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              act = xsdbool( lv_previous <= lv_current )
              msg =
                |{ i_property } ascending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
                && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

      lv_previous = lv_current.

    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_no_duplica_across_pages.
    DATA:
      lt_page_1 TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lt_page_2 TYPE tt_expected_index.

    lt_page_1 =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = i_json_page_1 ).

    lt_page_2 =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = i_json_page_2 ).

    LOOP AT lt_page_2 ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_page_2>).

      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
        act = xsdbool(
          line_exists(
            lt_page_1[
              ponumber = <ls_page_2>-ponumber
              itemno   = <ls_page_2>-itemno ] ) )
        msg =
          |Page 2 must not repeat page 1 row { <ls_page_2>-ponumber }/{ <ls_page_2>-itemno }| ).

    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_row_keys_match.
    DATA:
      lv_left_from  TYPE i,
      lv_left_to    TYPE i,
      lv_right_from TYPE i,
      lv_right_to   TYPE i,
      lv_left_po    TYPE string,
      lv_left_item  TYPE string,
      lv_right_po   TYPE string,
      lv_right_item TYPE string,
      lv_match      TYPE abap_bool.

    find_result_object_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_left
        i_index       = i_left_index
      IMPORTING
        e_object_from = lv_left_from
        e_object_to   = lv_left_to ).

    find_result_object_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_right
        i_index       = i_right_index
      IMPORTING
        e_object_from = lv_right_from
        e_object_to   = lv_right_to ).

    read_json_row_key(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_left
        i_object_from = lv_left_from
        i_object_to   = lv_left_to
      IMPORTING
        e_ponumber    = lv_left_po
        e_itemno      = lv_left_item ).

    read_json_row_key(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_right
        i_object_from = lv_right_from
        i_object_to   = lv_right_to
      IMPORTING
        e_ponumber    = lv_right_po
        e_itemno      = lv_right_item ).

    lv_match =
      xsdbool(
        lv_left_po = lv_right_po
        AND lv_left_item = lv_right_item ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lv_match
      exp = i_expected_match
      msg =
        |Unexpected row-key match result between row { i_left_index } and row { i_right_index }| ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_mime_fixture.
    DATA:
      lo_mime    TYPE REF TO if_mr_api,
      lo_conv    TYPE REF TO cl_abap_conv_in_ce,
      lv_content TYPE xstring.

    lo_mime =
      cl_mime_repository_api=>get_api( ).

    lo_mime->get(
      EXPORTING
        i_url = mc_mime_root && i_fixture
      IMPORTING
        e_content = lv_content
      EXCEPTIONS
        parameter_missing  = 1
        error_occured      = 2
        not_found          = 3
        permission_failure = 4
        OTHERS             = 5 ).

    IF sy-subrc <> 0
       OR lv_content IS INITIAL.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
        TYPE 'E'
        NUMBER '000'
        WITH
            |MIME fixture was not found: { mc_mime_root }{ i_fixture }|.

    ENDIF.

    lo_conv =
      cl_abap_conv_in_ce=>create(
        input    = lv_content
        encoding = 'UTF-8' ).

    lo_conv->read(
      IMPORTING
        data = rv_json ).

    FREE lv_content.

    CLEAR:
      lo_conv,
      lo_mime.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD find_results_array_bounds.
    DATA:
      lv_results_offset TYPE i,
      lv_json_length    TYPE i,
      lv_char           TYPE char1.

    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF '"results"'
      IN i_json
      MATCH OFFSET lv_results_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
        TYPE 'E'
        NUMBER '000'
        WITH 'Fixture does not contain the OData results property'.

    ENDIF.

    lv_json_length =
      strlen( i_json ).

    e_array_from =
      lv_results_offset + 9.

    WHILE e_array_from < lv_json_length.

      lv_char =
        i_json+e_array_from(1).

      IF lv_char = '['.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      e_array_from =
        e_array_from + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    IF e_array_from >= lv_json_length.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
        TYPE 'E'
        NUMBER '000'
        WITH 'Fixture results array start was not found'.

    ENDIF.

    e_array_to =
      find_matching_end(
        i_text  = i_json
        i_start = e_array_from
        i_open  = '['
        i_close = ']' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD find_result_object_bounds.
    DATA:
      lv_array_from TYPE i,
      lv_array_to   TYPE i,
      lv_position   TYPE i,
      lv_found      TYPE i,
      lv_char       TYPE char1,
      lv_value_end  TYPE i.

    IF i_index <= 0.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
        TYPE 'E'
        NUMBER '000'
        WITH 'Result row index must be greater than zero'.
    ENDIF.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_position = lv_array_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_array_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= lv_array_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char = i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.
        lv_position = lv_position + 1.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      lv_found = lv_found + 1.
      lv_value_end =
        read_json_value_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_position ).

      IF lv_found = i_index.
        IF lv_char <> '{'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
            TYPE 'E'
            NUMBER '000'
            WITH
                |Result row { i_index } is not a JSON object|.
        ENDIF.

        e_object_from = lv_position.
        e_object_to = lv_value_end - 1.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.

      lv_position = lv_value_end.

    ENDWHILE.

    RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
      MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
      TYPE 'E'
      NUMBER '000'
      WITH
          |Result row { i_index } was not found in the Gateway response|.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD build_expected_index.
    DATA:
      lv_array_from  TYPE i,
      lv_array_to    TYPE i,
      lv_position    TYPE i,
      lv_object_from TYPE i,
      lv_object_to   TYPE i,
      lv_ponumber    TYPE string,
      lv_itemno      TYPE string,
      lv_char        TYPE char1,
      ls_expected    TYPE ts_expected_index.

    CLEAR rt_index.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_position =
      lv_array_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_array_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= lv_array_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char =
        i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.

        lv_position =
          lv_position + 1.

        CONTINUE.

      ENDIF.

      IF lv_char <> '{'.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH
              |Unexpected character in results array at offset { lv_position }|.

      ENDIF.

      lv_object_from =
        lv_position.

      lv_object_to =
        find_matching_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_object_from
          i_open  = '{'
          i_close = '}' ).

      IF lv_object_to > lv_array_to.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH 'JSON result object exceeds the results array'.

      ENDIF.

      CLEAR:
        lv_ponumber,
        lv_itemno.

      read_json_row_key(
        EXPORTING
          i_json        = i_json
          i_object_from = lv_object_from
          i_object_to   = lv_object_to
        IMPORTING
          e_ponumber    = lv_ponumber
          e_itemno      = lv_itemno ).

      IF lv_ponumber IS INITIAL
         OR lv_itemno IS INITIAL.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH 'JSON row does not contain PONUMBER and ITEMNO'.

      ENDIF.

      CLEAR ls_expected.

      ls_expected-ponumber =
        lv_ponumber.

      ls_expected-itemno =
        lv_itemno.

      ls_expected-object_from =
        lv_object_from.

      ls_expected-object_to =
        lv_object_to.

      INSERT ls_expected
        INTO TABLE rt_index.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.

        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Duplicate JSON key; PO={ lv_ponumber }; |
            && |ITEMNO={ lv_itemno }| ).

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_object_to + 1.

    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_json_row_key.
    DATA:
      lv_position    TYPE i,
      lv_key_start   TYPE i,
      lv_key_end     TYPE i,
      lv_value_start TYPE i,
      lv_value_end   TYPE i,
      lv_key         TYPE string,
      lv_token       TYPE string,
      lv_char        TYPE char1.

    CLEAR:
      e_ponumber,
      e_itemno.

    lv_position =
      i_object_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < i_object_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char =
        i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.

        lv_position =
          lv_position + 1.

        CONTINUE.

      ENDIF.

      IF lv_char <> '"'.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH 'Invalid JSON object property name'.

      ENDIF.

      lv_key_start =
        lv_position.

      lv_key_end =
        read_json_string_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_key_start ).

      lv_key =
        unquote_json(
          i_token =
            substring(
              val = i_json
              off = lv_key_start
              len = lv_key_end - lv_key_start + 1 ) ).

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_key_end + 1 ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to
         OR i_json+lv_position(1) <> ':'.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH
              |JSON property { lv_key } has no colon|.

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position + 1 ).

      lv_value_start =
        lv_position.

      lv_value_end =
        read_json_value_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_value_start ).

      IF lv_value_end > i_object_to + 1.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH
              |JSON property { lv_key } exceeds its object boundary|.

      ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Only create a value substring for the two key properties.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      IF lv_key = 'PONUMBER'
         OR lv_key = 'ITEMNO'.

        lv_token =
          substring(
            val = i_json
            off = lv_value_start
            len = lv_value_end - lv_value_start ).

        IF lv_key = 'PONUMBER'.

          e_ponumber =
            unquote_json(
              i_token = lv_token ).

        ELSE.

          e_itemno =
            unquote_json(
              i_token = lv_token ).

        ENDIF.

        FREE lv_token.

        IF e_ponumber IS NOT INITIAL
           AND e_itemno IS NOT INITIAL.

          RETURN.

        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

      CLEAR lv_key.

      lv_position =
        lv_value_end + 1.

    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_first_result_key.
    DATA:
      lv_array_from  TYPE i,
      lv_array_to    TYPE i,
      lv_object_from TYPE i,
      lv_object_to   TYPE i.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_object_from =
      skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_array_from + 1 ).

    IF lv_object_from >= lv_array_to
       OR i_json+lv_object_from(1) <> '{'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_object_to =
      find_matching_end(
        i_text  = i_json
        i_start = lv_object_from
        i_open  = '{'
        i_close = '}' ).

    read_json_row_key(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json
        i_object_from = lv_object_from
        i_object_to   = lv_object_to
      IMPORTING
        e_ponumber    = e_ponumber
        e_itemno      = e_itemno ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_first_result_property.
    rv_token =
      read_result_property(
        i_json     = i_json
        i_index    = 1
        i_property = i_property ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_result_property.
    find_result_object_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json
        i_index       = i_index
      IMPORTING
        e_object_from = DATA(lv_object_from)
        e_object_to   = DATA(lv_object_to) ).

    DATA(lt_properties) =
      read_object_properties(
        i_json        = i_json
        i_object_from = lv_object_from
        i_object_to   = lv_object_to ).

    READ TABLE lt_properties ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_property>)
      WITH TABLE KEY name = i_property.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      rv_token = <ls_property>-token.
    ENDIF.

    rv_token =
      unquote_json(
        i_token = rv_token ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD compare_actual_page.
    LOOP AT it_actual_index ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_actual>).

      READ TABLE ct_expected_index
        ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_expected>)
        WITH TABLE KEY
          ponumber = <ls_actual>-ponumber
          itemno   = <ls_actual>-itemno.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Extra SEGW raw JSON row; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ <ls_actual>-ponumber }; ITEMNO={ <ls_actual>-itemno }| ).
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF <ls_expected>-matched = abap_true.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Duplicate SEGW raw JSON key across pages; |
            && |Fixture={ i_fixture }; PO={ <ls_actual>-ponumber }; |
            && |ITEMNO={ <ls_actual>-itemno }| ).
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      compare_raw_object(
        i_fixture       = i_fixture
        i_ponumber      = <ls_actual>-ponumber
        i_itemno        = <ls_actual>-itemno
        i_expected_json = i_expected_json
        i_expected_from = <ls_expected>-object_from
        i_expected_to   = <ls_expected>-object_to
        i_actual_json   = i_actual_json
        i_actual_from   = <ls_actual>-object_from
        i_actual_to     = <ls_actual>-object_to ).

      <ls_expected>-matched = abap_true.

    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD compare_raw_object.
    DATA:
      lt_expected TYPE tt_json_property,
      lt_actual   TYPE tt_json_property.

    lt_expected =
      read_object_properties(
        i_json        = i_expected_json
        i_object_from = i_expected_from
        i_object_to   = i_expected_to ).

    lt_actual =
      read_object_properties(
        i_json        = i_actual_json
        i_object_from = i_actual_from
        i_object_to   = i_actual_to ).

    LOOP AT lt_expected ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_expected>).

      READ TABLE lt_actual
        ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_actual>)
        WITH TABLE KEY name = <ls_expected>-name.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* XSA exposes a synthetic ID property that is not part of the migrated
* SEGW result contract and has no active UI consumer. Only this specific
* missing property is accepted. Every other missing property remains a
* parity failure.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
        IF ignore_missing_property(
             i_name = <ls_expected>-name ) = abap_true.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Missing SEGW raw JSON property; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ <ls_expected>-name }| ).
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      compare_raw_property(
        i_fixture        = i_fixture
        i_ponumber       = i_ponumber
        i_itemno         = i_itemno
        i_name           = <ls_expected>-name
        i_expected_token = <ls_expected>-token
        i_actual_token   = <ls_actual>-token ).

    ENDLOOP.

    LOOP AT lt_actual ASSIGNING <ls_actual>.
      IF NOT line_exists( lt_expected[ name = <ls_actual>-name ] ).
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Extra SEGW raw JSON property; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ <ls_actual>-name }| ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    FREE:
      lt_expected,
      lt_actual.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_object_properties.
    DATA:
      lv_position    TYPE i,
      lv_key_start   TYPE i,
      lv_key_end     TYPE i,
      lv_value_start TYPE i,
      lv_value_end   TYPE i,
      lv_key         TYPE string,
      lv_token       TYPE string,
      lv_char        TYPE char1.

    lv_position = i_object_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < i_object_to.

      lv_position = skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char = i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.
        lv_position = lv_position + 1.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_char <> '"'.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH
              |Invalid JSON property at offset { lv_position }|.
      ENDIF.

      lv_key_start = lv_position.
      lv_key_end = read_json_string_end(
        i_text  = i_json
        i_start = lv_key_start ).

      lv_key = unquote_json(
        i_token = substring(
          val = i_json
          off = lv_key_start
          len = lv_key_end - lv_key_start + 1 ) ).

      lv_position = skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_key_end + 1 ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to
         OR i_json+lv_position(1) <> ':'.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
          TYPE 'E'
          NUMBER '000'
          WITH
              |JSON property { lv_key } has no colon|.
      ENDIF.

      lv_value_start = skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_position + 1 ).

      lv_value_end = read_json_value_end(
        i_text  = i_json
        i_start = lv_value_start ).

      IF lv_key <> '__metadata'.
        lv_token = trim_text(
          i_text = substring(
            val = i_json
            off = lv_value_start
            len = lv_value_end - lv_value_start ) ).

        INSERT VALUE #(
          name  = lv_key
          token = lv_token
        ) INTO TABLE rt_properties.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
            TYPE 'E'
            NUMBER '000'
            WITH
                |Duplicate JSON property { lv_key }|.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR:
        lv_key,
        lv_token.

      lv_position = lv_value_end + 1.

    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD compare_raw_property.
    DATA:
      lv_expected_token TYPE string,
      lv_actual_token   TYPE string,
      lv_expected_text  TYPE string,
      lv_actual_text    TYPE string,
      lv_expected_len   TYPE i,
      lv_actual_len     TYPE i.

    lv_expected_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_expected_token ).

    lv_actual_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_actual_token ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Documented functional compatibility for XSA SQL NULL values
*
* Classic SEGW serializes initial ABAP character, decimal, and time
* values differently from XSA SQL NULL. IS_EQUIVALENT_NULL contains a
* field-specific allowlist. There is intentionally no global rule such
* as NULL = empty string or NULL = zero.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF is_equivalent_null(
         i_name           = i_name
         i_expected_token = lv_expected_token
         i_actual_token   = lv_actual_token ) = abap_true.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Normalize non-NULL CHANGEDTIME values
*
* XSA can omit leading zeroes while SEGW emits two-digit components.
* Both representations describe the same Edm.Time value:
*
*   "PT9H40M11S" = "PT09H40M11S"
*   "PT8H24M4S"  = "PT08H24M04S"
*
* Only CHANGEDTIME enters this normalization. The actual hour, minute,
* and second values remain strict.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF i_name = 'CHANGEDTIME'.
      lv_expected_token =
        normalize_odata_time(
          i_token = lv_expected_token ).

      lv_actual_token =
        normalize_odata_time(
          i_token = lv_actual_token ).
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* TODO_PARITY_STRICT_001 - temporary aggregate-order exception
*
* Aggregate segment order can currently differ between XSA and SEGW.
* Both values are decoded first. Content and multiplicity must still be
* identical after ordering is ignored.
*
* Accepted:
*   XSA="A;B;C", SEGW="C;A;B"
*
* Rejected:
*   XSA="A;B;C", SEGW="A;B"
*   XSA="A;A;B", SEGW="A;B"
*
* Remove this branch after aggregate ordering is deterministic and
* aligned between both implementations.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF is_unordered_aggregate_field(
         i_name = i_name ) = abap_true.

      lv_expected_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_expected_token ).

      lv_actual_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_actual_token ).

      IF equal_aggregate_content(
           i_expected = lv_expected_text
           i_actual   = lv_actual_text ) = abap_false.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Aggregate content differs; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ i_name }; XSA={ lv_expected_token }; |
            && |SEGW={ lv_actual_token }| ).
      ENDIF.

      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Compare decoded JSON string values
*
* Different JSON tokens can represent exactly the same string received
* by the UI. UNQUOTE_JSON removes the outer quotes and decodes supported
* JSON escape sequences.
*
* Accepted examples:
*   XSA="S \u0026 K",              SEGW="S & K"
*   XSA="King\u0027s College",    SEGW="King's College"
*   XSA="\/Date(1677240711333)\/",
*   SEGW="/Date(1677240711333)/"
*
* Rejected examples:
*   XSA="King's College", SEGW="Kings College"
*   XSA="A & B",          SEGW="A and B"
*
* NULL, numbers, booleans, arrays, and objects do not enter this branch
* and remain subject to strict token comparison below.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    lv_expected_len =
      strlen( lv_expected_token ).

    lv_actual_len =
      strlen( lv_actual_token ).

    IF lv_expected_len >= 2
       AND lv_actual_len >= 2
       AND lv_expected_token(1) = '"'
       AND lv_actual_token(1) = '"'.

      lv_expected_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_expected_token ).

      lv_actual_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_actual_token ).

      IF lv_expected_text <> lv_actual_text.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |String value differs; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ i_name }; XSA={ lv_expected_token }; |
            && |SEGW={ lv_actual_token }| ).
      ENDIF.

      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Strict comparison for every remaining JSON token
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lv_expected_token <> lv_actual_token.
      add_difference(
        i_text =
            |Raw value differs; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
          && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
          && |Field={ i_name }; XSA={ lv_expected_token }; |
          && |SEGW={ lv_actual_token }| ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* TODO_PARITY_FUNCTIONAL_NULL_001 - DOCUMENTED SEGW COMPATIBILITY
*
* XSA preserves database NULL and emits the JSON token null. Classic
* SEGW receives typed ABAP values and emits type-specific initial values:
*
*   initial character value -> ""
*   initial decimal value   -> "0.00"
*   initial TIMS value      -> "PT00H00M00S"
*
* The rules below are deliberately field-specific and one-directional:
*
*   XSA null -> approved SEGW initial representation
*
* Non-NULL XSA values remain strict. These examples are NOT accepted:
*
*   XSA="0.00", SEGW=null
*   XSA=null,   SEGW="1.00"
*   XSA=null,   SEGW="X"
*
* Reassess this allowlist whenever UI consumption or the OData contract
* changes. Do not extend it based on ABAP or EDM type alone.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  METHOD is_equivalent_null.
    rv_result = abap_false.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* TODO_PARITY_FUNCTIONAL_NULL_001
*
* Functional compatibility between XSA SQL NULL values and standard
* SEGW serialization of initial ABAP values.
*
* Background
* ----------
* XSA receives database NULL values directly from SAP HANA and emits:
*
*   null
*
* Classic SEGW receives typed ABAP values. Elementary ABAP value types
* do not preserve the distinction between an absent source value and an
* explicitly supplied initial value.
*
* Standard SEGW therefore serializes initial ABAP values as follows:
*
*   Character value  -> ""
*   Decimal value    -> "0.00"
*   TIMS value       -> "PT00H00M00S"
*
* Scope
* -----
* The rules below are deliberately field-specific. Do not replace them
* with global rules such as:
*
*   null = ""
*   null = "0.00"
*   null = "PT00H00M00S"
*
* NULL and an initial value can have different business meanings for
* other properties.
*
* The exceptions are also one-directional:
*
*   XSA null -> approved SEGW initial representation
*
* A non-NULL XSA value remains subject to strict comparison.
*
* The following examples are therefore NOT accepted:
*
*   XSA="0.00", SEGW=null
*   XSA=null,   SEGW="1.00"
*   XSA=null,   SEGW="X"
*
* Review this allowlist whenever UI consumption or the OData contract
* changes.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF i_expected_token <> 'null'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    CASE i_name.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Character properties serialized by SEGW as ""
*
* FULL_NAME
*   The current UI usage is display-only. Both XSA NULL and the SEGW
*   empty string produce an empty displayed value and do not change
*   active application control flow.
*
* DECIMAL_FORMAT
*   This exception applies to DECIMAL_FORMAT in the open-PO result.
*   The effective user decimal format is loaded separately from the
*   userDetails service.
*
* DATFM and USERNAME
*   No active UI consumer beyond metadata was identified.
*
* COSTCENTERDESC and MANAGEUNIT
*   These are aggregate/display properties. XSA NULL and SEGW empty
*   string both represent no aggregate value. Non-empty values still go
*   through the unordered aggregate comparison, including duplicates.
*
* NOTIF_24_HR
*   The UI activates the notification indicator only when the value is
*   exactly "X". Both NULL and "" therefore leave it inactive.
*
* REVIWED_BY
*   The property name intentionally follows the existing OData contract.
*   Both XSA NULL and the SEGW empty string render as an empty reviewer.
*   The property remains present in the response, so the UI does not
*   receive an omitted or undefined property.
*
* Accepted examples:
*
*   FULL_NAME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   DECIMAL_FORMAT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   DATFM:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   USERNAME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   NOTIF_24_HR:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   REVIWED_BY:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   COSTCENTERDESC:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   MANAGEUNIT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
* Rejected examples:
*
*   FULL_NAME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="John Smith"
*
*   NOTIF_24_HR:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="X"
*
*   REVIWED_BY:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="USER123"
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      WHEN 'COSTCENTERDESC'
        OR 'DATFM'
        OR 'DECIMAL_FORMAT'
        OR 'FULL_NAME'
        OR 'MANAGEUNIT'
        OR 'NOTIF_24_HR'
        OR 'REVIWED_BY'
        OR 'USERNAME'.

        rv_result =
          xsdbool(
            i_actual_token = '""'
          ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Decimal properties serialized by SEGW as "0.00"
*
* EDGEWTDAMT
*   Business.controller.js and TBS.controller.js explicitly normalize
*   an XSA NULL value to the string "0.00" before passing the amount to
*   the accrual calculation:
*
*     rData.EDGEWTDAMT === null
*       ? "0.00"
*       : rData.EDGEWTDAMT
*
*   XSA NULL and SEGW "0.00" therefore produce the same calculation
*   input and the same functional UI behavior.
*
* GRVALUE_USD
*   The UI already normalizes an XSA NULL amount to 0.00 before currency
*   formatting. SEGW "0.00" therefore produces the same visible result.
*
* INVVALUE_USD
*   This converted/display amount is initial in SEGW when XSA has no
*   source value. The exception is intentionally limited to INVVALUE_USD.
*   INVVALUE remains strict because it participates in accrual arithmetic.
*
* INVVALUE_CAL
*   No active UI consumer was identified for this calculated helper
*   property. XSA NULL and the initial SEGW decimal value "0.00" therefore
*   have no functional impact in the current UI. The exception remains
*   field-specific and must not be generalized to INVVALUE.
*
* LASTMONTHWRKCOMP
*   XSA NULL displays as an empty value. SEGW "0.00" can display as
*   0.00 in the detail view.
*
*   This small presentation difference is explicitly accepted because
