*   custom Gateway JSON serialization solely for this field would add
*   disproportionate implementation and maintenance complexity.
*
*   Backend presence information must remain separate from the numeric
*   value. A value of 0.00 must not be used as proof that the source
*   record existed.
*
* Accepted examples:
*
*   EDGEWTDAMT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   GRVALUE_USD:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   INVVALUE_USD:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   INVVALUE_CAL:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   LASTMONTHWRKCOMP:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
* Rejected examples:
*
*   EDGEWTDAMT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="1.00"
*
*   GRVALUE_USD:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="-1.00"
*
*   LASTMONTHWRKCOMP:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="10.00"
*
* The following direction is also rejected:
*
*   XSA="0.00", SEGW=null
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      WHEN 'EDGEWTDAMT'
        OR 'GRVALUE_USD'
        OR 'INVVALUE_CAL'
        OR 'INVVALUE_USD'
        OR 'LASTMONTHWRKCOMP'.

        rv_result =
          xsdbool(
            i_actual_token = '"0.00"'
          ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Time property serialized by SEGW as "PT00H00M00S"
*
* CHANGEDTIME
*   Initial ABAP TIMS value 000000 is also a valid midnight value.
*   Standard SEGW therefore serializes it as:
*
*     "PT00H00M00S"
*
*   XSA preserves an absent database value as:
*
*     null
*
*   The active UI primarily uses CHANGED_ON for the complete update
*   timestamp. The SEGW zero-time representation is therefore accepted
*   as a known compatibility deviation.
*
* Accepted example:
*
*   CHANGEDTIME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="PT00H00M00S"
*
* Rejected examples:
*
*   CHANGEDTIME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="PT10H15M00S"
*
*   CHANGEDTIME:
*     XSA="PT00H00M00S", SEGW=null
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      WHEN 'CHANGEDTIME'.

        rv_result =
          xsdbool(
            i_actual_token = '"PT00H00M00S"'
          ).

    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Normalize an OData V2 Edm.Time duration token
*
* XSA and SEGW can serialize the same time with different zero padding:
*
*   XSA:  "PT9H40M11S"
*   SEGW: "PT09H40M11S"
*
*   XSA:  "PT8H24M4S"
*   SEGW: "PT08H24M04S"
*
* Valid PT...H...M...S values are returned in canonical two-digit form.
* Invalid, unsupported, NULL, or unrelated tokens are returned unchanged,
* allowing the normal strict comparison to report a real difference.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  METHOD normalize_odata_time.
    DATA:
      lv_value       TYPE string,
      lv_remainder   TYPE string,
      lv_hour_text   TYPE string,
      lv_minute_text TYPE string,
      lv_second_text TYPE string,
      lv_hour        TYPE i,
      lv_minute      TYPE i,
      lv_second      TYPE i,
      lv_offset      TYPE i.

    rv_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_token ).

    lv_value =
      unquote_json(
        i_token = rv_token ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* A supported value must start with PT and contain H, M, and S.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF strlen( lv_value ) < 7
       OR lv_value(2) <> 'PT'
       OR lv_value NP 'PT*H*M*S'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_remainder =
      substring(
        val = lv_value
        off = 2 ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extract hours.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF 'H'
      IN lv_remainder
      MATCH OFFSET lv_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0
       OR lv_offset = 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_hour_text =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = 0
        len = lv_offset ).

    lv_remainder =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = lv_offset + 1 ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extract minutes.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF 'M'
      IN lv_remainder
      MATCH OFFSET lv_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0
       OR lv_offset = 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_minute_text =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = 0
        len = lv_offset ).

    lv_remainder =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = lv_offset + 1 ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extract seconds and reject any trailing content.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF 'S'
      IN lv_remainder
      MATCH OFFSET lv_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0
       OR lv_offset = 0
       OR lv_offset + 1 <> strlen( lv_remainder ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_second_text =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = 0
        len = lv_offset ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Reject non-numeric components before conversion.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lv_hour_text CN '0123456789'
       OR lv_minute_text CN '0123456789'
       OR lv_second_text CN '0123456789'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_hour =
      CONV i( lv_hour_text ).

    lv_minute =
      CONV i( lv_minute_text ).

    lv_second =
      CONV i( lv_second_text ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Validate ordinary time-of-day ranges.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lv_hour > 23
       OR lv_minute > 59
       OR lv_second > 59.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Canonical JSON token with two-digit components.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    rv_token =
        '"PT'
      && |{ lv_hour WIDTH = 2 PAD = '0' ALIGN = RIGHT }|
      && 'H'
      && |{ lv_minute WIDTH = 2 PAD = '0' ALIGN = RIGHT }|
      && 'M'
      && |{ lv_second WIDTH = 2 PAD = '0' ALIGN = RIGHT }|
      && 'S"'.
  ENDMETHOD.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* TODO_PARITY_CONTRACT_001 - SYNTHETIC XSA PROPERTY
*
* XSA exposes ID as a generated technical property. The migrated SEGW
* result contract identifies rows by PONUMBER and ITEMNO and does not
* expose ID. No active UI consumer uses the XSA ID value.
*
* Only a missing ID is accepted. Any other missing SEGW property remains
* a parity failure. Remove this exception if ID is later added to SEGW or
* becomes part of the functional UI contract.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  METHOD ignore_missing_property.
    rv_result =
      xsdbool( i_name = 'ID' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* TODO_PARITY_STRICT_001 - TEMPORARY EXCEPTION
* Aggregate values are compared without order. Content and multiplicity
* must still match. Remove this exception after aggregate ordering is
* aligned between XSA and SEGW.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  METHOD is_unordered_aggregate_field.
    CASE i_name.

      WHEN 'GLACCOUNT'
        OR 'COSTCENTER'
        OR 'COSTCENTERDESC'
        OR 'MANAGEUNIT'
        OR 'INTERNALORDER'
        OR 'ASSETID'
        OR 'PROFITCENTER'
        OR 'BBP_ACC_NO'
        OR 'DISTPERCENTAGE'
        OR 'RULETYPE'
        OR 'RULETYPETEXT'.

        rv_result =
          abap_true.

    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD equal_aggregate_content.
    DATA:
      lt_expected TYPE tt_string,
      lt_actual   TYPE tt_string.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Semantic unordered parity:
* - order of all segments is ignored;
* - leading, internal and trailing empty segments are preserved;
* - number of empty segments must still match.
*
* Therefore:
*   ;A;B     = A;B;
*   ;;A;B    = A;B;;
* but:
*   A;;B     <> A;B
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    lt_expected =
      split_aggregate_empty(
        i_value = i_expected ).

    lt_actual =
      split_aggregate_empty(
        i_value = i_actual ).

    IF lines( lt_expected ) <>
       lines( lt_actual ).

      FREE:
        lt_expected,
        lt_actual.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    SORT lt_expected
      BY table_line.

    SORT lt_actual
      BY table_line.

    rv_equal =
      xsdbool(
        lt_expected = lt_actual ).

    FREE:
      lt_expected,
      lt_actual.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD split_aggregate_empty.
    DATA:
      lv_length TYPE i,
      lv_start  TYPE i,
      lv_pos    TYPE i,
      lv_token  TYPE string.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Empty aggregate represents zero aggregate values, not one empty
* account-assignment slot. This follows the existing XSA convention.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF i_value IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_value ).

    WHILE lv_pos < lv_length.

      IF i_value+lv_pos(1) = ';'.

        lv_token =
          substring(
            val = i_value
            off = lv_start
            len = lv_pos - lv_start ).

        APPEND lv_token
          TO rt_tokens.

        CLEAR lv_token.

        lv_start =
          lv_pos + 1.

      ENDIF.

      lv_pos =
        lv_pos + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Always append the final segment. If the source ends in ';', this is
* deliberately an empty token.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    lv_token =
      substring(
        val = i_value
        off = lv_start
        len = lv_length - lv_start ).

    APPEND lv_token
      TO rt_tokens.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_json_string_end.
    DATA:
      lv_position TYPE i,
      lv_length   TYPE i,
      lv_char     TYPE char1,
      lv_escape   TYPE abap_bool.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).

    IF i_start >= lv_length
       OR i_text+i_start(1) <> '"'.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
        TYPE 'E'
        NUMBER '000'
        WITH 'JSON string does not start with quote'.

    ENDIF.

    lv_position =
      i_start + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_escape = abap_true.

        CLEAR lv_escape.

      ELSEIF lv_char = '\'.

        lv_escape =
          abap_true.

      ELSEIF lv_char = '"'.

        rv_end =
          lv_position.

        RETURN.

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
      MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
      TYPE 'E'
      NUMBER '000'
      WITH 'JSON string is not closed'.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_json_value_end.
    DATA:
      lv_position TYPE i,
      lv_length   TYPE i,
      lv_char     TYPE char1,
      lv_end      TYPE i.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).

    IF i_start >= lv_length.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
        TYPE 'E'
        NUMBER '000'
        WITH 'JSON value is missing'.

    ENDIF.

    lv_char =
      i_text+i_start(1).

    IF lv_char = '"'.

      lv_end =
        read_json_string_end(
          i_text  = i_text
          i_start = i_start ).

      rv_end =
        lv_end + 1.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    IF lv_char = '{'.

      lv_end =
        find_matching_end(
          i_text  = i_text
          i_start = i_start
          i_open  = '{'
          i_close = '}' ).

      rv_end =
        lv_end + 1.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    IF lv_char = '['.

      lv_end =
        find_matching_end(
          i_text  = i_text
          i_start = i_start
          i_open  = '['
          i_close = ']' ).

      rv_end =
        lv_end + 1.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_position =
      i_start.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','
         OR lv_char = '}'.

        rv_end =
          lv_position.

        RETURN.

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    rv_end =
      lv_length.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD find_matching_end.
    DATA:
      lv_position TYPE i,
      lv_length   TYPE i,
      lv_depth    TYPE i,
      lv_char     TYPE char1,
      lv_in_text  TYPE abap_bool,
      lv_escape   TYPE abap_bool.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).

    lv_position =
      i_start.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_in_text = abap_true.

        IF lv_escape = abap_true.

          CLEAR lv_escape.

        ELSEIF lv_char = '\'.

          lv_escape =
            abap_true.

        ELSEIF lv_char = '"'.

          CLEAR lv_in_text.

        ENDIF.

      ELSE.

        IF lv_char = '"'.

          lv_in_text =
            abap_true.

        ELSEIF lv_char = i_open.

          lv_depth =
            lv_depth + 1.

        ELSEIF lv_char = i_close.

          lv_depth =
            lv_depth - 1.

          IF lv_depth = 0.

            rv_end =
              lv_position.

            RETURN.

          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
      MESSAGE ID 'ZFI_DAS'
      TYPE 'E'
      NUMBER '000'
      WITH 'JSON object or array is not closed'.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD skip_whitespace.
    DATA:
      lv_length TYPE i,
      lv_char   TYPE char1.

    rv_position =
      i_position.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).

    WHILE rv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+rv_position(1).

      IF is_whitespace(
           i_char = lv_char ) = abap_false.

        RETURN.

      ENDIF.

      rv_position =
        rv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD is_whitespace.
    IF i_char = space
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf+0(1)
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf+1(1).

      rv_result =
        abap_true.

    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD trim_text.
    DATA:
      lv_start         TYPE i,
      lv_end           TYPE i,
      lv_length        TYPE i,
      lv_last_position TYPE i,
      lv_char          TYPE char1.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).

    lv_end =
      lv_length.

    WHILE lv_start < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_start(1).

      IF is_whitespace(
           i_char = lv_char ) = abap_false.

        EXIT.

      ENDIF.

      lv_start =
        lv_start + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    WHILE lv_end > lv_start.

      lv_last_position =
        lv_end - 1.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_last_position(1).

      IF is_whitespace(
           i_char = lv_char ) = abap_false.

        EXIT.

      ENDIF.

      lv_end =
        lv_end - 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    IF lv_end > lv_start.

      rv_text =
        substring(
          val = i_text
          off = lv_start
          len = lv_end - lv_start ).

    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD unquote_json.
    DATA:
      lv_token        TYPE string,
      lv_length       TYPE i,
      lv_last_offset  TYPE i,
      lv_inner_length TYPE i,
      lv_first_char   TYPE char1,
      lv_last_char    TYPE char1.

    lv_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_token ).

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        lv_token ).

    IF lv_length < 2.

      rv_value =
        lv_token.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_last_offset =
      lv_length - 1.

    lv_first_char =
      lv_token(1).

    lv_last_char =
      lv_token+lv_last_offset(1).

    IF lv_first_char <> '"'
       OR lv_last_char <> '"'.

      rv_value =
        lv_token.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_inner_length =
      lv_length - 2.

    rv_value =
      substring(
        val = lv_token
        off = 1
        len = lv_inner_length ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Avoid replacement scans in the normal case where the JSON value has
* no escaped characters.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF rv_value NS '\'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\u0026'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '&'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\u003C'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '<'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\u003c'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '<'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\u003E'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '>'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\u003e'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '>'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\u0022'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '"'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\u0027'
      IN rv_value
      WITH ''''.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\/'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '/'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\"'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '"'.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\n'
      IN rv_value
      WITH cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\r'
      IN rv_value
      WITH cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf+0(1).

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\t'
      IN rv_value
      WITH cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '\\'
      IN rv_value
      WITH '\'.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD add_difference.
    mv_difference_count =
      mv_difference_count + 1.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Count every mismatch, but retain a bounded diagnostic sample. This
* prevents failures with many missing rows from allocating huge logs.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lines( mt_differences ) >= mc_max_log.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND i_text
      TO mt_differences.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_no_differences.
    DATA:
      lv_text   TYPE string,
      lv_line   TYPE string,
      lv_shown  TYPE i,
      lv_hidden TYPE i.

    IF mv_difference_count = 0.

      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        act = abap_true ).

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_shown =
      lines(
        mt_differences ).

    lv_hidden =
      mv_difference_count
      - lv_shown.

    lv_text =
        |DAS XSA vs SEGW OData parity failed. |
      && |Difference count: { mv_difference_count }.|
      && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
      && |--- First { lv_shown } difference(s) ---|.

    LOOP AT mt_differences INTO lv_line.

      lv_text =
          lv_text
        && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
        && lv_line.

    ENDLOOP.

    IF lv_hidden > 0.

      lv_text =
          lv_text
        && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
        && |--- { lv_hidden } further difference(s) omitted ---|.

    ENDIF.

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
      msg = lv_text ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltc_dash_pure_branches IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD setup.
    mo_cut = NEW #( ).
    mo_cut->mt_exchrt = VALUE #(
      ( source_curr = 'USD' target_curr = 'USD' exchange_rate = '1' )
      ( source_curr = 'EUR' target_curr = 'USD' exchange_rate = '1.5' )
      ( source_curr = 'JPY' target_curr = 'USD' exchange_rate = '0.01' )
      ( source_curr = 'VND' target_curr = 'USD' exchange_rate = '0.001' )
      ( source_curr = 'EUR' target_curr = 'JPY' exchange_rate = '150' ) ).
    ms_request = VALUE #(
      mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
      parameter = VALUE #( email = 'owner@example.invalid' pstype = '0' )
      paging = VALUE #( skip = 2 top = 3 top_requested = abap_true
                        count_requested = abap_true ) ).
    ms_key = VALUE #( ponumber = '4500000001' itemno = '00010'
                      pstype = '0' ).
    ms_detail = VALUE #(
      ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno
      effective_pstype = '0' pocurrency = 'EUR' grossvalue = '100'
      companycode = '1000' cocdecurr = 'EUR'
      supplierid = '0000000123' orderquantity = '2.999'
      popreparer = '  PREP@EXAMPLE.INVALID  '
      poowner_email = 'owner@example.invalid' ).
    ms_context = VALUE #(
      review_day = 7
      details = VALUE #( ( ms_detail ) )
      accounts = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno
          glaccount = '4000' costcenter = '123'
          manageunit = 'MU1' internalorder = '42' assetid = '7'
          profitcenter = 'PC1' bbp_acc_no = '01' distpercentage = '100' ) )
      history = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno
          gr_value = '80.129' grvalue_usd = '120.18'
          invvalue = '20' invvalue_cocdecurr = '20' invvalue_usd = '30' ) )
      users = VALUE #(
        ( email = ms_detail-poowner_email username = 'OWNER'
          full_name = 'Fixture Owner' decimal_format = 'X' datfm = '1' ) )
      audit = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno amount = '17.25' ) )
      exclusions = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno
          ruletype = 'CC' ruletypetext = 'Cost Center' ) ) ).
    CLEAR ms_row.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_row.
    ms_row = mo_cut->get_row(
      is_key = ms_key is_context = ms_context
      is_request = ms_request i_decimal_format = 'X' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_rejected.
    TRY.
        mo_cut->validate_request( ms_request ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( msg = 'Expected request rejection' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          act = lx_error->if_t100_message~t100key-msgid exp = 'ZFI_DAS' ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          act = lx_error->if_t100_message~t100key-msgno exp = i_msgno ).
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Arithmetic and cached currency conversion
  METHOD currency_factors.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_currency_factor( 'JPY' ) exp = 100 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_currency_factor( 'VND' ) exp = 100 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_currency_factor( 'EUR' ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_currency_factor( space ) exp = 1 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD usd_truncates_source.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
        i_amount = '1.239' i_source_currency = 'EUR' )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '1.85' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
        i_amount = '-1.239' i_source_currency = 'EUR' )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '-1.85' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
        i_amount = '12.349' i_source_currency = 'JPY' )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '12.34' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD usd_keeps_precision.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
        i_amount = '1.239' i_source_currency = 'EUR'
        i_keep_precision = abap_true )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '1.86' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
        i_amount = '12.349' i_source_currency = 'VND'
        i_keep_precision = abap_true )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '1.23' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD zero_needs_no_rate.
    CLEAR mo_cut->mt_exchrt.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
        i_amount = 0 i_source_currency = 'ZZZ' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
        i_amount = '0.009' i_source_currency = 'ZZZ' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD missing_rate_rejected.
    TRY.
        DATA(lv_amount) = mo_cut->convert_to_usd(
          i_amount = 1 i_source_currency = 'ZZZ' ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( msg = 'Missing cached rate must fail' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          act = lx_error->if_t100_message~t100key-msgno exp = '017' ).
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD rate_target_key.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_exchange_rate( 'EUR' )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount_precision( '1.5' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_exchange_rate(
        i_source_currency = 'EUR' i_target_currency = 'JPY' )
      exp = 150 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Context component mapping
  METHOD detail_mapping.
    ms_detail-pocurrency = 'JPY'.
    ms_detail-poowner_email = '  OWNER@EXAMPLE.INVALID  '.
    mo_cut->apply_detail(
      EXPORTING is_detail = ms_detail CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-ponumber exp = ms_key-ponumber ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-itemno exp = ms_key-itemno ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-supplierid exp = '123' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-povalue exp = 10000 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-pstype exp = '0' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-orderquantity
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '2.99' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-po_preparer exp = 'prep@example.invalid' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-poowner_email exp = 'owner@example.invalid' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD account_mapping.
    mo_cut->apply_account(
      EXPORTING is_account = ms_context-accounts[
        ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno ]
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-glaccount exp = '4000' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-costcenter exp = '123' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-costcenterdesc ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-manageunit exp = 'MU1' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-internalorder exp = '42' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-assetid exp = '7' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-profitcenter exp = 'PC1' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-bbp_acc_no exp = '01' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-distpercentage exp = '100' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD history_mapping.
    mo_cut->apply_history(
      EXPORTING is_history = ms_context-history[
        ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno ]
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-grvalue exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '80.12' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-grvalue_cocdecurr exp = ms_row-grvalue ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-grvalue_usd exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '120.18' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-invvalue exp = 20 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-invvalue_cocdecurr exp = 20 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-invvalue_usd exp = 30 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD user_mapping_guard.
    mo_cut->apply_user(
      EXPORTING is_user = ms_context-users[ email = ms_detail-poowner_email ]
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-username exp = 'OWNER' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-full_name exp = 'Fixture Owner' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-decimal_format exp = 'X' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-datfm exp = '1' ).
    mo_cut->apply_user(
      EXPORTING is_user = VALUE #( username = 'IGNORE' )
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-username exp = 'OWNER' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD exclusion_mapping.
    ms_row-costcenterdesc = 'Must be cleared'.
    ms_row-costcenter = '123'.
    mo_cut->apply_exclusion(
      EXPORTING is_exclusion = ms_context-exclusions[
        ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno ]
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-ruletype exp = 'CC' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-ruletypetext exp = 'Cost Center' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-costcenterdesc ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-costcenter exp = '123' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Row assembly: no get_context/get_results or database-backed loaders
  METHOD row_missing_detail.
    CLEAR: ms_context-details, mo_cut->mt_exchrt.
    read_row( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD row_material_and_audit.
    read_row( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-username exp = 'OWNER' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-glaccount exp = '4000' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-status exp = 'Pending' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-lastmonthwrkcomp exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '17.25' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-amountaccrued exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '60.12' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-amountaccrued_usd exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '90.18' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-povalue_usd exp = 150 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-pstype_text exp = 'Material' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-accrualmethod exp = 'Straight Line' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-po_closure exp = 'false' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-poclosure_flag ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-last_notif_on ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-notif_24_hr ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-ruletype ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD row_service_wtd.
    ms_detail-effective_pstype = '9'.
    ms_detail-serviceamount = '45.67'.
    ms_detail-servicepercentage = '45.6'.
    ms_context-details = VALUE #( ( ms_detail ) ).
    ms_request-mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    read_row( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-pstype_text exp = 'Service' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-accrualmethod exp = 'Work To Date' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-straightlinemthd exp = 'false' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '45.67' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt_usd exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '68.51' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-totwrkcompper exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_percentage( '45.6' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-amountaccrued exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '25.67' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-lastmonthwrkcomp ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD row_optional_context.
    ms_context = VALUE #( details = VALUE #( ( ms_detail ) ) review_day = 7 ).
    ms_request-mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    read_row( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-ponumber exp = ms_key-ponumber ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-status exp = 'Pending' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-povalue_usd exp = 150 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-username ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-glaccount ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-invvalue ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-amountaccrued ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD row_exclusion.
    ms_request-mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
    read_row( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-ruletype exp = 'CC' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-ruletypetext exp = 'Cost Center' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-costcenterdesc ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-costcenter exp = '123' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt_char ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-last_notif_on ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD row_uses_complete_key.
    INSERT VALUE #( BASE ms_detail itemno = '00020' grossvalue = 999 )
      INTO TABLE ms_context-details.
    INSERT VALUE #( ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = '00020' invvalue = 999 )
      INTO TABLE ms_context-history.
    INSERT VALUE #( ponumber = '4500000002' itemno = ms_key-itemno glaccount = 'WRONG' )
      INTO TABLE ms_context-accounts.
    read_row( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-povalue exp = 100 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-invvalue exp = 20 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-glaccount exp = '4000' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD row_closed_status.
    ms_request-mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    ms_context-statuses = VALUE #(
      ( ponumber = ms_key-ponumber itemno = ms_key-itemno
        status = 'Other' po_closure = 'TrUe' reviewed_by = 'Reviewer'
        wtd_amount = 12 last_month_amount = 34 ) ).
    read_row( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-status exp = 'Reviewed' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-po_closure exp = 'true' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-poclosure_flag exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-reviwed_by exp = 'Reviewer' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-edgewtdamt exp = 12 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-lastmonthwrkcomp exp = 34 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-markasreviewed exp = 'false' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-changed_on ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-changeddate ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-changedtime ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD missing_notification_clears.
    ms_row-last_notif_on = '20260101120000'.
    ms_row-notif_24_hr = abap_true.
    mo_cut->apply_notification(
      EXPORTING is_notification = VALUE #( )
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-last_notif_on ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-notif_24_hr ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Scope parsing
  METHOD company_wildcard.
    DATA(lt_scope) = mo_cut->get_company_codes( ' * ' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_scope exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_company_scope(
        ( wildcard = abap_true ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = mo_cut->get_company_codes( ', , ' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD company_mixed_values.
    DATA(lt_scope) = mo_cut->get_company_codes( 'ab01, AB01,*,1000' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_scope exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_company_scope(
        ( value = 'AB01' ) ( value = '*' ) ( value = '1000' ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD cost_null_sentinel.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( ' NULL,NULL ' )
      exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_cost_center_scope(
        ( is_null = abap_true ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( ', , ' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD cost_null_and_alpha.
    DATA(lt_scope) = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( 'NULL,123,0000000123,ab1' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_scope exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_cost_center_scope(
        ( is_null = abap_true ) ( value = '0000000123' ) ( value = 'AB1' ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD cost_mixed_wildcard.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( '*' )
      exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_cost_center_scope(
        ( wildcard = abap_true ) ) ).
    DATA(lt_scope) = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( '*,NULL' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = lt_scope exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_cost_center_scope(
        ( value = '*' ) ( is_null = abap_true ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD management_scope.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_management_units( '*' )
      exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_management_scope(
        ( wildcard = abap_true ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_management_units( 'NULL,0012,0012,*' )
      exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_management_scope(
        ( is_null = abap_true ) ( value = '0012' ) ( value = '*' ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = mo_cut->get_management_units( '' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_y_character_path.
    ms_row-totwrkcompamt = CONV #( '1234567.89' ).
    ms_row-amountaccrued = CONV #( '1491846.48' ).
    ms_row-amountaccrued_usd = CONV #( '901339.30' ).

    mo_cut->apply_odata_format(
      EXPORTING
        i_mode           = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
        i_decimal_format = 'Y'
      CHANGING
        cs_result        = ms_row ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt_char exp = '1 234 567,89' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-amountaccrued_char exp = '1 491 846,48' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-amountaccrued_usd_char exp = '901 339,30' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt exp = CONV #( '1234567.89' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-amountaccrued exp = CONV #( '1491846.48' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ms_row-amountaccrued_usd exp = CONV #( '901339.30' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Validation and request adjustment
  METHOD valid_request_modes.
    mo_cut->validate_request( ms_request ).
    ms_request-mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    ms_request-parameter-pstype = '9'.
    ms_request-parameter-poowner = abap_true.
    mo_cut->validate_request( ms_request ).
    ms_request-parameter-pstype = '*'.
    mo_cut->validate_request( ms_request ).
    ms_request-mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
    ms_request-parameter-pstype = '?'.
    mo_cut->validate_request( ms_request ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->get_pstype( ms_request ) exp = '*' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD invalid_mode.
    ms_request-mode = '?'.
    assert_rejected( '005' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD missing_email.
    CLEAR ms_request-parameter-email.
    assert_rejected( '006' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD invalid_owner.
    ms_request-parameter-poowner = '?'.
    assert_rejected( '007' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD invalid_pstype.
    ms_request-parameter-pstype = '?'.
    assert_rejected( '008' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD negative_paging.
    ms_request-paging-skip = -1.
    assert_rejected( '009' ).
    ms_request-paging-skip = 0.
    ms_request-paging-top = -1.
    ms_request-paging-top_requested = abap_false.
    assert_rejected( '009' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD missing_filter_text.
    ms_request-filter-filter_select_options = VALUE #(
      ( property = 'CompanyCode' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '1000' ) ).
    assert_rejected( '010' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD adjust_default_request.
    ms_request-parameter-email = '  OWNER@EXAMPLE.INVALID  '.
    ms_request-orderby = VALUE #( ( property = 'ponumber' ) ( property = 'itemno' ) ).
    DATA(ls_original) = ms_request.
    DATA(ls_adjusted) = mo_cut->adjust_request( ms_request ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ls_adjusted-parameter-email exp = 'owner@example.invalid' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ls_adjusted-orderby ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ls_adjusted-paging exp = ls_original-paging ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_request exp = ls_original ).
    ms_request-parameter-email = ' * '.
    ls_adjusted = mo_cut->adjust_request( ms_request ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ls_adjusted-parameter-email exp = '%' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD adjust_generic_request.
    ms_request-orderby = VALUE #( ( property = 'AmountAccrued' descending = abap_true ) ).
    ms_request-filter-filter_string = `COMPANYCODE = '1000'`.
    DATA(ls_adjusted) = mo_cut->adjust_request( ms_request ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ls_adjusted-orderby exp = ms_request-orderby ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ls_adjusted-filter exp = ms_request-filter ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ls_adjusted-paging-skip ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ls_adjusted-paging-top ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ls_adjusted-paging-top_requested ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true( act = ls_adjusted-paging-count_requested ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Status and calculation boundaries: no implicit current-date branches
  METHOD closed_overrides_status.
    mo_cut->determine_status(
      EXPORTING is_status = VALUE #( po_closure = 'TrUe' status = 'Pending' )
                i_review_day = 7
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-status exp = 'Reviewed' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false( act = mo_cut->is_closed( 'false' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false( act = mo_cut->is_closed( 'X' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD unknown_status_cleared.
    ms_row-status = 'Reviewed'.
    mo_cut->determine_status(
      EXPORTING is_status = VALUE #( status = 'Other' ) i_review_day = 7
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-status ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD reviewed_without_date.
    ms_row-status = 'Reviewed'.
    mo_cut->determine_status(
      EXPORTING is_status = VALUE #( status = 'Reviewed' ) i_review_day = 7
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-status ).
    ms_row-changeddate = '20260101'.
    ms_row-changedtime = '120000'.
    mo_cut->determine_review(
      EXPORTING is_status = VALUE #( status = 'Reviewed' )
                i_mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over i_review_day = 7
      CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-changeddate ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-changedtime ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD material_resets_work.
    CLEAR mo_cut->mt_exchrt.
    ms_row = VALUE #( totwrkcompamt = 99 totwrkcompamt_usd = 98
                      totwrkcompper = 97 straightlinemthd = abap_true
                      accrualmethod = 'WTD' ).
    mo_cut->calculate_accrual(
      EXPORTING is_detail = ms_detail CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt_usd ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-totwrkcompper ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-straightlinemthd ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals( act = ms_row-accrualmethod exp = 'STL' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD unsupported_method.
    CLEAR mo_cut->mt_exchrt.
    ms_detail-effective_pstype = '9'.
    ms_row = VALUE #( accrualmethod = 'Other' totwrkcompamt = 99
                      totwrkcompamt_usd = 98 totwrkcompper = 97
                      straightlinemthd = abap_true ).
    mo_cut->calculate_accrual(
      EXPORTING is_detail = ms_detail CHANGING cs_result = ms_row ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-accrualmethod ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-totwrkcompamt_usd ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-totwrkcompper ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial( act = ms_row-straightlinemthd ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD straight_line_missing_dates.
    CLEAR mo_cut->mt_exchrt.
    DATA(ls_calc) = mo_cut->calculate_straight_line(
      i_po_value = 100 i_po_currency = 'ZZZ'
      i_work_start_date = '00000000' i_work_end_date = '20260131' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ls_calc exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_calculation(
        accrualmethod = 'STL' straightlinemthd = abap_true ) ).
    ls_calc = mo_cut->calculate_straight_line(
      i_po_value = 100 i_po_currency = 'ZZZ'
      i_work_start_date = '20260101' i_work_end_date = '00000000' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = ls_calc exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_calculation(
        accrualmethod = 'STL' straightlinemthd = abap_true ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD accrued_exact_boundary.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_accrued_amount(
        i_pstype = '9' i_total_work_amount = 10
        i_gr_value = 999 i_invoice_value = 10 ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      act = mo_cut->calculate_accrued_amount(
        i_pstype = '0' i_total_work_amount = 999
        i_gr_value = '10.01' i_invoice_value = 10 )
      exp = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_amount( '0.01' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.
